The vehicular network is modelled as a graph, where nodes represent CAVs and RSUs, and edges encode radio-link characteristics. An offline Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) formulation provides optimal relay decisions used as supervision for training an edge-aware Graph Isomorphism Network with Edge Features (GINE). Extensive experiments on realistic urban datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves near-optimal connectivity performance, recovering up to 11.3% connectivity gain, while reducing execution time by orders of magnitude (up to 100 x speed-up) compared to MILP. The framework enables scalable and real-time network control, making it suitable for smart city and Industry 4.0 deployments.

</details>


### [242] [JEPA-WAM: Connecting Generated Visual Instructions to World Action Models through JEPA Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20277)
*Tianbin Liu,Jian Zhu,Taiyi Su,Jianjun Zhang,Chong Ma,Zitai Huang,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JEPA-WAM，通过为每条文本指令合成多样化的视觉指令（任务完成图），并用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1编码为紧凑目标token以条件化视频与动作专家，从而增强世界动作模型的指令跟随能力，在自建真机基准上显著超越π0与Fast-WAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）虽具备较强机器人操作能力，但仅以文本指令为条件时指令跟随能力有限。作者认为部分原因在于机器人学习数据的结构性失衡：视觉-动作轨迹丰富，而语言标注稀疏且重复，使策略倾向于从视觉上下文和运动规律中识别任务，而非真正对指令进行语义落地。

Method: 提出JEPA-WAM：使用现成的文本到图像生成器（无需训练）根据文本指令采样多张任务完成图像，作为与指令语义对齐但外观/布局可能不同的视觉目标参考；随后用冻结的V-JEPA 2.1编码器提取这些参考的密集目标表征，并压缩为紧凑目标token；通过交叉注意力同时条件化视频专家与动作专家。此外构建了覆盖分布内、分布外场景与分布外指令三类设置的真机指令跟随基准。

Result: 在该基准上，JEPA-WAM在分布内、分布外场景、分布外指令三种设置下分别取得87.3%、74.5%、80.9%的成功率，较π0与Fast-WAM分别至少高出10.0、27.3、14.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过引入随机生成的视觉指令参考并借助JEPA语义表征进行条件化，可有效缓解机器人学习数据中语言稀疏导致的对指令语义落地不足问题，显著提升WAM的指令跟随与泛化能力。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated strong robotic manipulation capabilities by augmenting pretrained video generative models with action experts. However, current WAMs still show limited instruction-following ability when conditioned solely on text instructions. We argue that this limitation stems in part from a structural imbalance in robot-learning data: rich visual-action trajectories are often paired with sparse and repetitive language annotations, allowing policies to identify tasks from visual context and motion regularities rather than grounding the instruction itself. To address this limitation, we introduce JEPA-WAM, which augments each text instruction with a bank of stochastically generated visual instructions, providing diverse visual cues for instruction following. Specifically, JEPA-WAM uses an off-the-shelf text-to-image generator to sample multiple task-completion images conditioned on the text instruction, without training the generator. Although these generated images may differ from the current visual scene in appearance and layout, they remain semantically aligned with the instruction and serve as visual goal references. To focus on task-level semantics beyond appearance, we encode these references with a frozen V-JEPA 2.1 encoder. The resulting dense goal representations are compressed into compact goal tokens that condition both the video and action experts through cross-attention. We further construct a real-robot instruction-following benchmark covering in-distribution, out-of-distribution scene, and out-of-distribution instruction settings. On this benchmark, JEPA-WAM achieves success rates of 87.3%, 74.5%, and 80.9% in these three settings, outperforming π0 and Fast-WAM by at least 10.0, 27.3, and 14.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [243] [AgentPProf: Semantic Profiler for Long Horizon AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20301)
*Yusheng Zheng,Chaokun Chang,Yu Mao,Tianyuan Wu,Yuxi Huang,Tao Ma,Wenan Mao,Shuyi Cheng,Andi Quinn,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出语义操作栈模型与递归操作分割方法，构建了智能体性能分析工具AgentPProf，将长周期智能体轨迹聚合为pprof兼容的性能剖面，实现分层归因、问题定位与成本优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体日益承担跨越数天到数周的长周期活动，开发者需要确定故障发生位置、触发不安全效应的原因以及哪些任务消耗最多预算，从而提升质量、安全性与成本效率。现有智能体可观测工具聚焦单次执行的调试与追踪，缺乏跨运行、长期的性能剖析能力，难以在规模化场景下回答上述问题。因此智能体可观测性需要剖析（profiling）而非仅调试，但剖析面临挑战：责任实体是"诊断认证""比较分支"这类任务意图而非代码路径，且缺乏稳定的聚合标识符。

Method: 提出语义操作栈（semantic operation stack）模型，将剖析适配到智能体轨迹：用统一操作表示所有活动，用操作栈替代运行时调用栈，实现不同粒度的分层归因。基于智能体任务占据连续区间并可分解为子任务的观察，引入递归操作分割（recursive operation segmentation），在任务边界处递归切分轨迹。据此实现AgentPProf剖析器，将智能体轨迹聚合为与pprof兼容的性能剖面，支持火焰图可视化与分析。

Result: 在CodeTraceBench上，AgentPProf相对于人工标注达到0.764的B³ F1分数；在三个问题定位基准上，其剖面将MAP最高提升56%；表明其能以可接受的剖析开销有效完成资源归因、问题定位并帮助优化token成本。

Conclusion: 智能体可观测性应从调试扩展到长期跨运行的性能剖析，AgentPProf通过语义操作栈与递归操作分割实现了轨迹的分层归因，在问题定位与token成本优化上取得显著效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly orchestrate long-running activities with users, tools, and system resources for days and weeks. To improve agent quality, safety, and cost efficiency, developers need to determine where failures happen, what triggers unsafe effects, and which tasks consume the most budget, then optimize those tasks. In systems software, profiling answers similar questions by aggregating resource consumption and attributing it to responsible code paths to identify hotspots. Yet existing agent observability tools focus on per-execution debugging and tracing rather than cross-run, long term profiling, making these questions difficult to answer at scale. Agent observability needs profiling, not only debugging, but profiling agents is challenging: the responsible entities are task intent like diagnose authentication, compare branches rather than code paths, and lack stable identifiers for aggregation. We propose a semantic operation stack model that adapts profiling to agent trajectories. Uniform operations represent all activities, and operation stacks replace the runtime call stack, enabling hierarchical attribution at different granularities. We observe that an agent's task occupies a contiguous span and decomposes into subtasks, so we introduce recursive operation segmentation, which recursively splits trajectories at task boundaries. AgentPProf is a profiler that aggregates agent trajectories into pprof-compatible profiles, enabling flame graph visualization and analysis. AgentPProf reaches 0.764 $B^3$ F1 against human annotations on CodeTraceBench. On three problem-localization benchmarks, the profile raises MAP by up to 56%, demonstrating that it effectively attributes resources, locates problems, and helps optimize token cost at practical profiling cost. AgentPProf is available at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/agentsight.

</details>


### [244] [Diagnose, Recover, Certify: Task Readiness under Hidden Dynamics Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20304)
*Nguyen Viet Tuan Kiet,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"休眠动力学漂移下的任务就绪性"决策问题，并给出证据门控匹配脉冲传输（EGMPT）方法，用于在有限、任务无关交互预算下统一主动变化诊断与变化后控制恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署控制策略可能掩盖后果性动力学变化：执行器若长期未被策略激励，其失效不会影响当前任务，但对未来尚未指定的任务却至关重要，因此需要一种能在任务身份揭示前进行诊断与就绪评估的机制。

Method: 提出EGMPT，一种基于干预的贝叶斯程序：通过共享的匹配响应表征，将故障定位与执行器有效性估计耦合，保持诊断可靠性并将定位证据转化为恢复相关的不确定性，再传播到任务条件策略选择与就绪性认证，以显式权衡期望性能、置信度与回退使用。

Result: 在跨多个仿真器的休眠执行器基准上评估，采用将诊断与能力恢复分离的协议，以就绪覆盖率、选择性风险、交互成本及回报综合评分部署表现，并识别出传输证据具有决定性作用的故障制度。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在任务无关的有限交互预算下实现休眠动力学漂移的诊断与恢复，并通过就绪认证支持显式权衡性能、置信度与回退的部署决策。

Abstract: A deployed control policy can conceal consequential dynamics changes: an actuator may lose effectiveness without affecting the current task when the policy rarely excites it, despite being critical for a future task that has not yet been specified. We introduce task readiness under dormant dynamics drift, a decision problem that unifies active change diagnosis and post-change control recovery under a limited, task-agnostic interaction budget. An agent must identify whether and where local dynamics have changed, use a small number of informative interactions to characterize the change before downstream task identity is revealed, and subsequently provide each candidate task with either a recovered policy and a calibrated lower bound on its achievable return or an abstention decision to a safe fallback. We propose Evidence-Gated Matched-Pulse Transport, an intervention-based Bayesian procedure that couples fault localization with estimation of actuator effectiveness through a shared matched-response representation, thereby preserving diagnostic reliability while converting localized evidence into recovery-relevant uncertainty. This uncertainty is propagated to task-conditioned policy selection and readiness certification, enabling deployment decisions that explicitly trade off expected performance, confidence, and fallback use. We evaluate the resulting framework on a diverse suite of dormant-actuator benchmarks spanning multiple simulators, under a protocol that separates diagnosis from capability recovery, scores deployment by readiness coverage, selective risk, and interaction cost as well as return, and identifies the fault regimes in which transported evidence is decisive.

</details>


### [245] [NeuSOGA3D: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable 3D Geometric Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20323)
*Qingde Li,Qingqi Hong,Zihan Li,Jie Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NeuSOGA3D混合框架，将神经感知先验与显式符号几何推理结合，把点云逐步转换为控制多边形、隐式样条场、截面与体积放样等CAD兼容的符号表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无组织点云的三维重建仍具挑战。神经隐式方法虽精度高，但几何被编码在隐空间中，可解释性差，难以在工程工作流中复用。因此需要一种兼顾学习感知能力与显式符号几何推理、可解释且CAD兼容的重建方法。

Method: NeuSOGA3D将点云投影到主正交平面，从投影观测构建符号隐式样条表示，并通过保形CSG操作融合生成粗略视觉外壳；随后通过截面分解与基于Partial Shape-Preserving Splines的体积重建恢复几何细节，逐步将观测转化为显式符号实体（控制多边形、隐式样条场、截面、体积放样）。

Result: 在ModelNet40全部四十个类别上的实验表明，该框架能从多样点云观测中恢复具有结构意义且CAD兼容的几何表示，验证了学习感知与符号几何推理结合的有效性。

Conclusion: NeuSOGA3D展示了将学习感知与符号几何推理结合用于可解释几何智能的潜力，为工程可复用的点云重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Three-dimensional reconstruction from unorganized point clouds remains a challenging problem in computer vision, geometric modeling, and computer-aided design. While neural implicit methods achieve impressive reconstruction accuracy, geometry is typically encoded in latent representations that limit interpretability and reuse within engineering workflows.
  We present NeuSOGA3D (Neuro-Symbolic Geometric Abstraction in 3D), a hybrid framework that combines learned perceptual priors inherited from NeuSOGA with explicit symbolic geometric reasoning. The method projects point clouds onto principal orthographic planes, constructs symbolic implicit spline representations from the resulting observations, and fuses them through shape-preserving constructive solid geometry operations to generate a coarse visual hull. Additional geometric detail is recovered through cross-sectional decomposition and volumetric reconstruction using Partial Shape-Preserving Splines.
  Unlike conventional neural implicit approaches, NeuSOGA3D progressively transforms observations into explicit symbolic entities, including control polygons, implicit spline fields, cross-sections, and volumetric lofts. Experiments on all forty categories of the ModelNet40 benchmark demonstrate the ability of the framework to recover structurally meaningful and CAD-compatible geometric representations from diverse point-cloud observations. The results highlight the potential of combining learned perception with symbolic geometric reasoning for explainable geometric intelligence.

</details>


### [246] [Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation: From Natural-Language Traffic Rules to PROLOG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20334)
*May Myo Zin,Wachara Fungwacharakorn,Ken Satoh,Katsumi Nitta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对交通法规自然语言向Prolog逻辑转换中的歧义与语义欠规范问题，提出结构化四阶段法律翻译框架S4L，在20条真实交通规则基准上取得75%的正确形式化率，优于NL→Prolog（60%）和LE→Prolog（55%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通法规面向人类解读，依赖共享背景知识与灵活措辞，天然带有歧义性、上下文依赖和语义欠规范，这与Prolog等计算推理引擎所需的精确、显式逻辑结构相冲突。已有翻译路径（自然语言直译、Logical English中转）难以可靠地保留法律语义，因此需要一种能显式恢复隐含语义结构的翻译方法。

Method: 研究评估了两个基线方法：自然语言到Prolog（NL→Prolog）与Logical English到Prolog（LE→Prolog），并提出推理引导的翻译框架S4L→Prolog。S4L在单一引导提示中依次执行四个阶段：语义角色抽取、场景补全、逻辑映射、Prolog规则生成，从而将原始交通规则直接转换为可执行逻辑，无需人工干预。以20条真实交通规则构建基准，从语法有效性、语义正确性和逻辑完整性三个维度评估各方法。

Result: S4L→Prolog取得最高准确率，正确形式化75%的规则；NL→Prolog为60%，LE→Prolog为55%。定性分析显示，S4L在捕捉隐式因果关系、道义模态（deontic modality）和例外结构方面比基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 结构化推理提示能显著提升自然语言到逻辑翻译的可靠性，表明其在法律与安全攸关应用中具有实用价值，可作为提升法规可执行化与自动化推理可信度的有效路径。

Abstract: Traffic regulations are written for human interpretation and therefore rely on shared background knowledge and flexible phrasing, which inherently introduce ambiguity, context dependence, and semantic underspecification. These linguistic characteristics conflict with the precision required by computational reasoning engines such as Prolog, which demand explicit logical structure. This study evaluates two baseline translation approaches, Natural Language to Prolog ($NL\rightarrow Prolog$) and Logical English to Prolog ($LE\rightarrow Prolog$), and introduces a new reasoning-guided translation framework called Structured Four-Stage Legal Translation ($S4L\rightarrow Prolog$). The proposed S4L framework performs semantic role extraction, scene completion, logical mapping, and Prolog rule generation within a single guided prompt, enabling direct translation of raw traffic rules into executable logic without human intervention. A benchmark consisting of twenty real-world traffic rules was used to evaluate each approach in terms of syntactic validity, semantic correctness, and logical completeness. $S4L\rightarrow Prolog$ achieves the highest accuracy, correctly formalizing 75 percent of the rules, while $NL\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 60 percent and $LE\rightarrow Prolog$ reaches 55 percent. Qualitative analysis further shows that S4L captures implicit causal relations, deontic modality, and exception structure more reliably than the baselines. These results demonstrate that structured reasoning prompts can substantially improve the reliability of natural-language-to-logic translation for legal and safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [247] [A Qualitative Model for Reasoning about Path and Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20349)
*Abhishek Jaiswal,Zoe Falomir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为Camelot Jr.积木拼图游戏提出一种混合定性模型，将数学质心稳定性逻辑与定性推理求解器结合，以支持可解释的类人玩家引导与空间技能训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理能力与STEM领域表现高度相关，而游戏是训练儿童空间能力的有效媒介；但游戏要提供类人化辅导和引导，需要AI智能体能从空间事件中进行常识推断。定性推理模型以符号表示推理，可将游戏状态转化为可解释反馈，因此适合该任务。

Method: 以Camelot Jr.为应用场景，该游戏要求搭建多层桥梁连接两座塔上的角色，玩家需保证平台稳定、规划路径并使用全部积木。作者提出一种混合定性模型，将数学化的质心稳定性逻辑集成到定性求解器中，以处理领域所需的精确物理判断。

Result: 构建了一个面向Camelot Jr.的混合定性推理系统，能够结合质心稳定性计算与定性求解，对游戏中的空间事件进行推理并生成可解释的反馈。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于在Camelot Jr.中开展空间技能训练，并推动以人为中心、可解释的游戏智能体发展。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning abilities correlate strongly with performance in STEM fields. Games offer a compelling medium for training these critical skills in developing children who have a natural proclivity for play. However, to facilitate human-like tutoring and player guidance, these games require an AI agent capable of making commonsense inferences from spatial events. Qualitative reasoning (QR) models appear to be a suitable framework for these application domains. As these models reason in symbolic representations, they can seamlessly translate game states into interpretable feedback for human-like player guidance. This paper introduces a hybrid qualitative model designed for Camelot Jr., a block-puzzle game that requires constructing multi-level bridges to connect two avatars stationed on separate towers. The game poses a challenge for the player, who must make platforms stable, plan their path, and ensure they use all the provided blocks. To handle the precise physics required by the domain, we integrate a mathematical center-of-mass stability logic to guide our qualitative solver. Our work facilitates spatial skill training in Camelot Jr. and contributes to the development of human-centric, explainable game-playing agents.

</details>


### [248] [Generating Heterogeneous 3D Geological Microstructures from 2D Images via a Stable Diffusion-Adversarial Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20358)
*Ali Aouf,Eric Laloy,Bart Rogiers,Christophe De Vleeschouwer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合生成框架，用去噪扩散模型替代SliceGAN，在无3D真值条件下以对抗损失训练，从2D图像重建复杂非均质3D微观结构，切片伪影更少且统计描述符吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黏土与胶凝材料的物理性质表征依赖3D成像，但3D成像昂贵、不易获得，且对某些材料存在技术限制。虽有基于GAN的方法（如SliceGAN）可从易获取的2D图像重建3D体数据，但其在同质各向同性与各向异性体系表现良好，却难以刻画更复杂非均质微观结构的精细细节，因此需要替代性生成框架。

Method: 提出一种融合去噪扩散模型的混合方法：利用扩散模型在稳定性与生成质量上的优势；由于缺乏3D真值，将标准去噪损失替换为对抗损失，从而在实验中实现稳定训练。模型由2D切片监督，生成3D微观结构。

Result: 该模型能够生成复杂度各异（同质与异质）的微观结构，切片伪影极少，生成结果的相分数与结构描述符与真值高度吻合。

Conclusion: 以对抗损失驱动的扩散式生成框架为从2D图像重建3D微观结构提供了可行方案，在保持训练稳定性的同时改善了复杂非均质体系的细节还原能力，可服务于黏土与胶凝材料的性质模拟。

Abstract: Characterizing the physical properties of clay and cementitious materials matters across many fields, from materials science to geological waste disposal. Property simulation typically calls for 3D imaging, which is expensive, not always accessible, and technically limited for certain materials. Recent progress in deep generative models offers a way around this, reconstructing 3D volumes from the more easily acquired 2D images.
  Among GAN-based methods for 3D microstructure generation, SliceGAN has shown strong results for homogeneous isotropic and anisotropic systems. It struggles, however, to capture the finer detail of more complex heterogeneous microstructures, which motivates alternative generative frameworks.
  We introduce a hybrid approach that draws on the stability and generation quality of denoising diffusion models. Since no 3D ground truth is available, we replace the standard denoising loss with an adversarial loss, which yields a stable training process in our experiments. We show that the resulting model generates microstructures of varying complexity with minimal slice artefacts and close agreement with ground-truth phase fractions and structural descriptors.

</details>


### [249] [The Organization of Inference: Information, Resource Constraints, and AI Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20449)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文通过受控工作流实验揭示：推理的经济价值不仅取决于规模（token上限），还取决于工作流与信息结构；在低容量下直接执行优于受限规划，但容量提升后任务知情的规划反超，且多数额外用量来自下游执行阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理的经济价值取决于算力容量与任务信息在AI生产各阶段的分布方式，但这一组织性边际问题缺乏在可外部验证任务上的受控证据，需要厘清"规模"与"工作流/信息结构"对生产价值的相对作用。

Method: 在外部可验证的软件工程任务上进行受控工作流实验。设置两组匹配的资源面板，对比直接执行与信息受限规划，并在logical-token上限12,000与24,000下测量成功率；另开展严格只读的规划实验，操纵规划者是否可见任务issue；同时记录资源使用、binding rate与下游执行占比。

Result: 直接执行在12,000与24,000 tokens下成功率均为59.6%；信息受限规划的成功率由36.2%升至51.2%，劣势收窄15.0个百分点（95%任务簇bootstrap区间4.2–25.8）。在12,000 tokens下，可见issue的规划比隐藏issue高约16个百分点；任务知情规划在12,000 tokens时比直接执行低约10个百分点，但在24,000 tokens时反超29.6个百分点。直接执行用量远低于两个上限；规划工作流的binding rate由46.2%降至0.8%，下游执行贡献了总用量增量的89.9%。

Conclusion: 规模决定系统可用容量，而工作流与信息结构塑造其生产价值：容量不足时规划的信息约束造成劣势，容量充足且任务信息可得时规划带来显著增益，且增量主要消耗在下游执行环节。

Abstract: The economic value of inference depends on how capacity and task information are distributed across stages of AI production. We study these organizational margins using controlled workflow experiments on externally verified software-engineering tasks. In two matched resource panels, direct execution records the same success rate of 59.6 percent at logical-token ceilings of 12,000 and 24,000, while success under information-constrained planning rises from 36.2 to 51.2 percent. The planning disadvantage narrows by 15.0 percentage points (95 percent task-cluster bootstrap interval: 4.2 to 25.8). A strict read-only planning campaign varies whether the planner sees the task issue. At 12,000 tokens, issue access raises success by about 16 percentage points over issue-hidden planning. Compared with direct execution, task-informed planning is about 10 points lower at 12,000 tokens; at 24,000 tokens, it shows a 29.6-point advantage. In the resource panels, direct execution uses substantially less than either ceiling, while the planning workflow's binding rate falls from 46.2 to 0.8 percent and downstream execution accounts for 89.9 percent of the increase in total use. Scale determines the capacity available to a system; workflow and information structure shape the productive value

</details>


### [250] [SkillAA: Attribution-Guided Skill-Graph Updating with Targeted Validation and Rollback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20455)
*Ziqiao Shang,Ling-Yue Ge,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SkillAA 框架，将外部技能以统一图结构表示，通过失败归因定位并局部修复技能，配合双层门控验证更新，在冻结语言模型上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部技能方法多直接从失败轨迹编辑技能，缺乏从观察到失败到可编辑位置的结构化路由；技能图也未充分利用语义边界、对象地址与拓扑依赖，难以支持技能检索、定向更新与范围化验证。

Method: SkillAA 在统一图结构中表示技能的适用性、执行与组合，使同一结构支持技能选择、归因引导修复与更新验证；通过对比成功与失败执行，将候选修复路由到具体图对象，仅更新所选局部结构，并用 Local Gates 与 Big Gates 在提交前筛选候选变更。

Result: 使用 gpt-5.6-sol 时，SkillAA 在 SearchQA、LiveMath 和 DocVQA 上分别达到 81.5%、66.7% 和 91.2%，在所有主要设置中均取得最高观测均值。

Conclusion: 结果支持归因引导的图编辑与图范围化验证的有效性，表明结构化技能路由与局部更新可提升冻结语言模型的技能优化效果。

Abstract: External skills provide domain procedures without parameter updates, but existing methods often edit skills directly from failed rollouts without structured routing from an observed failure to an editable location; existing skill graphs also underuse semantic boundaries, object addresses, and topological dependencies for skill retrieval, targeted updating, and scoped validation. We introduce SkillAA (Skill Abductive Attribution), a structured skill-optimization framework for frozen language models. It represents skill applicability, execution, and composition in a unified graph, allowing the same structure to support skill selection, attribution-guided repair, and update validation. SkillAA contrasts successful and failed executions to route candidate repairs to specific graph objects, updates only the selected local structure, and uses Local and Big Gates to screen candidate changes before commitment. With gpt-5.6-sol, SkillAA reaches 81.5%, 66.7%, and 91.2% on SearchQA, LiveMath, and DocVQA, respectively, and attains the highest observed mean in every main setting. These results support the utility of attribution-guided graph editing and graph-scoped validation.

</details>


### [251] [How Do Agent Harnesses Create Value? Planning Information and Release Control in Stateful LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20474)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在τ²-bench的两个Retail实验和一个Airline试点中，预写任务特定计划（Fixed）相比同词数打乱策略文本（Sham）将oracle验证成功率提升7.17个百分点，增益集中于高复杂度任务；只读终端验证器以每episode不足1美分的额外成本拒绝61%的oracle无效episode，同时误拒17%正确episode。规划与验证何者更重要取决于错误接受的损失权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究agent harness（规划指导、执行组织、完成检查）各组件对成功率、错误接受（erroneous acceptance）与成本的影响，并厘清规划指导内容本身与验证组件各自的贡献，以便在考虑错误接受代价时做出组件取舍。

Method: 在τ²-bench上开展两个Retail实验和一个Airline试点。核心对照为预写任务特定计划（Fixed）与词数匹配的打乱策略文本（Sham），以隔离指导内容的作用；并采用只读终端验证器进行完成检查。共265个匹配单元，使用90%任务聚类bootstrap区间估计效应。

Result: Fixed相比Sham使oracle验证成功率提升7.17个百分点（90%任务聚类bootstrap区间1.15–13.36个百分点），增益集中在高复杂度任务。只读终端验证器拒绝61%的Retail oracle无效episode，误拒17%正确episode，每episode额外成本不足1美分。组件重要性随错误接受损失的设定而变化。

Conclusion: 规划指导内容本身对成功率有实质贡献，验证器则以极低成本显著减少错误接受。低责任/低错误接受代价时规划增益占主导；高责任时验证器避免的假通过占主导，且独立验证器能以远低于完整“规划+验证”栈的成本捕获几乎全部假通过收益。

Abstract: Agent harnesses supply planning guidance, organize execution, and check completion. We study how these components affect success, erroneous acceptance, and cost in two Retail experiments and an Airline pilot in $τ^2$-bench. The primary comparison pairs prewritten task-specific plans (Fixed) with shuffled policy text matched in word count (Sham), isolating the contribution of guidance content. Across 265 matched cells, Fixed improves oracle-verified success by 7.17 percentage points (90\% task-clustered bootstrap interval, 1.15--13.36 points), with gains concentrated in higher-complexity tasks. A read-only terminal verifier rejects 61\% of Retail oracle-invalid episodes while withholding 17\% of correct ones, at less than one cent of additional cost per episode. Which component matters more depends on the loss assigned to erroneous acceptance: at low liability the planning gain dominates; at high liability the verifier's avoided false passes dominate---and a standalone verifier captures nearly all the false-pass benefit of the full planning-plus-verification stack at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [252] [SoL-Pi: Recursively Scaling Auto-Research Loops for Efficient Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20519)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Sensen Gao,Qihang Cao,Yitong Li,Mingchen Zhuge,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Ping Luo,Jiawang Bian,Lei Zhu,Ligeng Zhu,Enze Xie,Song Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出SoL-Pi——一种在harness层面对编码智能体进行自动发现与优化的方法，通过跨多环境的大规模auto-research循环筛选出四种可复用机制，在保持性能的同时显著降低token消耗与API成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编码智能体从受监督的代码补全转向无人值守的持续探索，其工作形态从孤立的预测扩展为包含推理、工具调用与反馈的长轨迹。此时token效率成为扩展递归自我改进（RSI）的关键瓶颈，需要一种可规模化、可迁移的优化路径。

Method: 采用RSI启发式思路，在harness层进行优化：将auto-research循环扩展至数量更多、种类更多样的环境中执行harness rollout；在该规模下筛选出可迁移至开发环境之外的改进，最终保留四种机制构成SoL-Pi，覆盖动作执行、上下文压缩、观测处理和委托式阅读。

Result: 在51任务的EdgeBench评测中，SoL-Pi在GPT-5.6 Sol与Opus 5上性能与Pi相当，同时将记录的token流量降低44.7%–49.0%，API成本降低约三分之一。折算每小时节省约\$8.75–\$13.50（相对原生Codex与Claude Code harness）、\$4.36–\$5.71（相对Pi）。

Conclusion: 在harness层面进行规模化自动研究可产出可复用、可迁移的改进，使自动harness发现朝向生产级成果推进，并在不牺牲性能的前提下实现显著的token与成本节约。

Abstract: As coding agents move from supervised code completion to unattended, around-the-clock exploration, their work expands from isolated predictions into long trajectories of reasoning, tool use, and feedback. Token efficiency therefore becomes important for scaling recursive self-improvement. We take an RSI-inspired approach at the harness layer, scaling auto-research loops across increasingly numerous and diverse environments for harness rollouts. At this scale, the process yields reusable improvements that transfer beyond their development setting, moving automated harness discovery toward production-level outcomes. Four mechanisms survive selection and form SoL-Pi, spanning action execution, context compaction, observation handling, and delegated reading. On the 51-task EdgeBench evaluation, SoL-Pi achieves performance comparable to Pi across GPT-5.6 Sol and Opus 5 while reducing recorded token traffic by 44.7-49.0% and API cost by about one third. In other words, estimated hourly savings are \$8.75-\$13.50 relative to native Codex and Claude Code harnesses, and \$4.36-\$5.71 relative to Pi.

</details>


### [253] [FreqCondNorm: Towards Cross-domain Predictive Maintenance through a Frequency-Conditioned Transformer Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20535)
*Zaynab Raounak,Camille LHermine,Zhiguo Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FreqCondNorm——基于Transformer并引入FiLM式频率条件归一化层的预测性维护架构，统一融合1 Hz~100 kHz异构时序；在五个公开数据集上以掩码自编码+对比学习预训练。故障诊断上CWRU达99.2%（较CNN提升6.4个百分点）、MFPT零样本82.1%，跨采样频率迁移性强；但RUL预测未见改进，提示预训练目标与RUL任务不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习预测性维护模型跨机器、跨工况迁移性差，尤其在标注数据稀缺、信号采样频率跨越五个数量级（1 Hz~100 kHz）时更为突出。现有方法难以在单一模型中统一处理如此异构的时序数据。

Method: 提出FreqCondNorm，一种基于Transformer的架构，核心是FiLM式频率条件归一化层，用以在单一模型内统一异构时序。在CWRU、MFPT、UOC18、PRONOSTIA、CMAPSS五个公开预测性维护数据集上进行预训练，采用掩码自编码与对比学习，并结合均衡域采样。

Result: 故障诊断任务上，CWRU准确率99.2%（较CNN提升6.4个百分点），MFPT零样本准确率82.1%，表明跨采样频率迁移能力强。但在剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测上未取得改进，说明预训练目标与RUL任务之间存在不匹配。

Conclusion: FreqCondNorm通过频率条件归一化有效统一跨采样频率的异构时序，在故障诊断与跨域零样本迁移上表现优越；但方法对RUL预测无效，预训练与RUL目标的不匹配值得后续研究。

Abstract: Deep learning predictive maintenance models suffer from poor transferability across machines and operating conditions, especially when labelled data are scarce and signals span five orders of magnitude in sampling frequency (1 Hz to ~100 kHz). We propose FreqCondNorm, a Transformer-based architecture that introduces a FiLM-style frequency-conditioned normalization layer to unify heterogeneous time-series within a single model. The architecture is pretrained on five public predictive maintenance datasets (CWRU, MFPT, UOC18, PRONOSTIA, CMAPSS) using masked auto-encoding and contrastive learning with balanced domain sampling. On fault diagnosis, the model achieves 99.2% accuracy on CWRU (+6.4 pp over CNN) and 82.1% zero-shot accuracy on MFPT, demonstrating strong transfer across sampling frequencies. However, the approach does not improve remaining useful life prediction, suggesting a mismatch between pretraining and RUL objectives that warrants future investigation.

</details>


### [254] [Refuse, Decompose, Refresh: A Claim-Safe Protocol for Closed-Loop AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20538)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种“claim-safe”评估协议，通过 Refuse（弃权）、Decompose（分解报告）、Refresh（刷新参考）三种操作，将闭环系统中的可复现性与主张有效性解耦，并在仅聚合数据的模拟器中实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估即使完全可复现，仍可能支持错误主张；在闭环系统中，策略决定访问状态、可观测组件及哪些故障留下可测痕迹，该风险尤为突出，因此需要一个能区分“可复现”与“可正当主张”的协议。

Method: 提出claim-safe协议，含三动作：Refuse（当缺少干净参考流或匹配的运行时比较时弃权）、Decompose（将协议执行、操作性误接受与结构性假设分别报告，而非单一PASS/FAIL）、Refresh（将分布漂移告警视为使参考图失效并重算的请求，而非故障证据）。在仅聚合数据的模拟器中实例化：24个策略组件、3种需求情景、2类故障掩蔽族、独立的开发与保留种子；预注册保留集含1,440案例与21,600划分行。

Result: 仅55/72情景-组件单元通过参考准入，其中54/55保持运行时准入，弃权本身成为结果的一部分；代表性组件中稳定误接受为0/20，在冻结0.20规则下单侧精确95%上界为0.1391。在准入单元内，受影响干净流量比名义故障单元比例更具预测力：540个单元-臂行嵌套于20个组件簇，单元减流量的负对数似然差为每行0.1264 nats，95%组件簇区间[0.0593, 0.1918]。漂移日志显示“null”必须相对于参考：干净故障零流在三种情景下分别触发15/15、0/15、14/15告警，仅中间情景与冻结检测器参考一致。

Conclusion: 与其提供通用阈值，本文贡献一个可执行契约，将可观测支撑、统计校准与可正当主张三者关联起来，强调弃权与参考相对性是评估结果不可分割的组成部分。

Abstract: An AI evaluation can be perfectly reproducible and still support the wrong claim. This risk is acute in closed-loop systems: policy determines visited states, observable components, and which failures leave a measurable trace. We propose a claim-safe protocol with three actions. Refuse: abstain when a clean reference stream or matched runtime comparison lacks support. Decompose: report protocol execution, operational false admission, and structural hypotheses separately rather than as one PASS/FAIL label. Refresh: treat distribution-shift alarms as requests to invalidate and recompute a reference map, not as fault evidence. We instantiate the protocol in an aggregate-only simulator with 24 policy components, three demand regimes, two fault-mask families, and independent development and heldout seeds. The preregistered heldout contains 1,440 cases and 21,600 partition rows. Only 55/72 regime-component units were reference-admitted and 54/55 remained runtime-admitted, making abstention part of the result. Stable false admission was 0/20 represented components, with a one-sided exact 95% upper bound of 0.1391 under a frozen 0.20 rule. Within admitted units, affected clean traffic outpredicted nominal fault-cell fraction: across 540 unit-arm rows nested in 20 component clusters, the cell-minus-traffic negative-log-likelihood difference was 0.1264 nats per row, with a 95% component-cluster interval of [0.0593, 0.1918]. A drift log shows why "null" must be reference-relative: clean fault-null streams triggered 15/15, 0/15, and 14/15 alarms across three regimes, while only the middle regime matched the frozen detector reference. Rather than a universal threshold, we contribute an executable contract linking observable support, statistical calibration, and justified claims.

</details>


### [255] [Language-model groups overstate consensus when replaying human deliberation on a reasoning task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20543)
*Tengfei Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究以同一评分代码对比100组人类Wason任务讨论与匹配的LLM智能体小组，发现智能体小组的共识率显著更高，且该差异在不同评分定义、排除提前停止及去除可记忆答案后依然稳健，但模拟共识并不反映集体准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全共识率常被当作集体认知的指标，但其数值高度依赖"参与"与"最终状态"的操作化定义；作者希望建立一个明确评分口径的框架，用于评估LLM模拟小组能否复现人类审议结果。

Method: 回放100组留出的人类Wason任务小组，并构建匹配的LLM智能体小组：依据每位参与者讨论前答案植入一个信念锚定智能体，使用相同代码对人类和智能体打分。设置两种敏感性分析路径——基于提交（n=98）与参与匹配（n=45），并进一步做无提前停止、去除可记忆答案的重参数化检验。

Result: 人类评分定义下共识率估计在24.0%–57.0%之间；约五分之一参与者从未发帖，而智能体几乎总是发帖。两种敏感性分析中智能体小组共识度更高：提交比较中聊天与推理模式差距为34.0和43.9个百分点，参与匹配比较中为34.1和44.4个百分点，两条路径差异收敛在0.5个百分点内。去除提前停止与可记忆答案后差距仍存在，推理模式小组近乎一致同意，但多集中于错误答案。

Conclusion: 模拟共识不追踪集体准确性，信念锚定智能体小组是此情境下人类小组结果分布的有偏估计量；研究提供了评分口径明确的基础，用于评估模拟小组对人类审议结果的估计。

Abstract: Full-consensus rates are often treated as indicators of collective cognition, yet depend on how participation and final states are operationalized. We replayed 100 held-out human Wason groups with matched large language model (LLM) agent groups, seeding one belief-anchored agent per participant's pre-discussion answer and scoring agents and people with the same code. Across human scoring definitions, estimates ranged from 24.0% to 57.0%; about one fifth of participants never posted, whereas agents almost always did. Agent groups remained more consensual in two post-unblinding sensitivity analyses: the submit-based comparison (n = 98) yielded gaps of 34.0 and 43.9 percentage points for chat and reasoning modes, and the participation-matched comparison (n = 45) yielded gaps of 34.1 and 44.4 points. These complementary routes reduced different measurement asymmetries yet converged within 0.5 percentage points. The gap persisted without early stopping and under a reparameterization removing the memorizable answer; reasoning-mode groups then agreed nearly unanimously, mostly on incorrect answers. Simulated consensus did not track collective accuracy, and belief-anchored agent groups were biased estimators of the human group-outcome distribution in this setting. These analyses provide a scoring-explicit basis for assessing simulated-group estimates of human deliberative outcomes.

</details>


### [256] [Limits of Confidence in Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20581)
*Russ Webb,Amitis Shidani,Alice Bizeul,Dan Busbridge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 离散扩散（remasking 与 uniform-state 采样器）在每步同时写入多个 token 位置，但仅当所写位置在已固定 token 条件下相互独立时，该步才与训练分布匹配；一般兴趣域中 token 存在固有依赖，逐位置分布之积无法匹配依赖组，且逐位置边缘分布无法判定组是否依赖。作者在可解析联合分布的合成任务 ScanAndAdd 上验证：置信度排序所写的任意≥2个未定位置组均为依赖组，生成分布总变差达采样噪声下限的 29 倍，而逐样本指标为 1.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散生成模型（remasking、uniform-state 采样）每步并行写入多个位置，各位置从其边缘分布采样，并据此决定写哪些位置。然而像素、音素、词等一般领域的 token 之间存在固有依赖，这种"逐位置独立采样 + 并行写入"的做法可能在原理上无法复现训练分布。本文旨在从理论上刻画这一失配的根源，并给出可量化的验证。

Method: 理论分析：证明一步采样匹配训练分布的充要条件是所写位置在已固定 token 条件下条件独立；证明逐位置分布之积无法匹配依赖组；并构造两个具有相同逐位置边缘分布但在取值组合出现方式上不同的联合分布，说明边缘分布不能判定依赖。实验验证：在 ScanAndAdd 合成任务上（联合分布有闭式解），检查置信度排序所写的每一组≥2个未定位置是否依赖，并用总变差（TV）对比生成分布与采样噪声下限，同时报告逐样本指标。

Result: 在 ScanAndAdd 上，置信度排序每步所写的任意≥2个未定位置组均被验证为依赖组；生成分布的总变差为采样噪声下限的 29 倍，而逐样本指标（per-sample metrics）为 1.0，说明逐样本评估无法揭示分布层面的失配。

Conclusion: 并行多位置写入的离散扩散采样在存在 token 依赖的域中无法匹配训练分布，其根本原因是忽略了待写位置间的条件依赖，且这种失配无法由逐位置边缘分布检测，也难以通过逐样本指标发现。评估离散扩散模型应关注联合分布层面的度量（如相对采样噪声下限的总变差），而非仅依赖逐样本指标。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion, including remasking and uniform-state samplers, generate a sequence by writing multiple token positions per step, drawing each from a per-position distribution and choosing which positions to write from those same distributions. For domains of general interest (pixels, phonemes, or words) there are inherent dependencies between tokens. We show that a step matches the training distribution only when the positions it writes are conditionally independent given the tokens already fixed, that no product of per-position distributions can match a dependent group, and that per-position distributions do not determine whether a group is dependent: two joint distributions can have identical per-position marginals while differing in which combinations of values occur. On ScanAndAdd, a synthetic task whose joint distribution is available in closed form, we verify that every group of two or more undetermined positions a confidence ranking writes is dependent, and measure the generated distribution to be $29\times$ the sampling-noise floor total variation while per-sample metrics are $1.0$.

</details>


### [257] [PAA: The Probabilistic Allen Algebra: A Generative and Complete Probabilistic Extension of Allen's Interval Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20634)
*Julian Eggert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率Allen代数（PAA），用高斯端点分布生成区间关系概率，构成对经典Allen区间代数的生成式完整扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Allen区间代数的13种基本关系是精确边界上的清晰谓词，无法处理语言、感知、数据库或不确定历史中时间、时长与边界的模糊性，也难以表达"just before""roughly during"等渐变语义。

Method: 以时间点为高斯分布，区间取高斯中点与截断高斯时长；将每种关系表示为同一概率空间中的边界排序谓词。点-点关系化为误差函数，点-区间及区间-区间关系化为线性不等式诱导的多元高斯象限概率；通过容差带使接触关系（meets、starts、finishes、equals）获得正测度。

Result: 在单一容差下13种关系构成真划分，容差趋于零时退化为清晰Allen代数；粗粒度谓词（precedence、overlap、containment）为叶节点并集，其概率为叶概率之和，且在区间收缩为点时保持层级（13→5→3）。每种关系可进一步分解为相关感知的时间原语，符合CIDOC CRM思想。

Conclusion: PAA具有尺度不变性，能区分"shortly before"等渐变表达与接触关系；所有结果经蒙特卡洛验证，并以开源、可测试的Python包发布。

Abstract: Allen's interval algebra is a qualitative calculus for temporal relations, but its thirteen base relations are crisp predicates over exact interval boundaries. This is inadequate for temporal information from language, perception, databases, or uncertain histories, where times, durations, and boundaries are uncertain and expressions such as "just before" or "roughly during" have graded meaning. We develop the probabilistic Allen algebra (PAA): a generative and complete extension in which relation probabilities are derived from distributions over interval boundaries rather than assigned as scores. Time points are Gaussian; intervals have Gaussian midpoints and truncated-Gaussian durations. Every relation is a boundary-ordering predicate in one common probability space: point-point relations reduce to error functions, and point-interval and interval-interval relations to multivariate Gaussian orthant probabilities induced by linear inequalities. Contact relations (meets, starts, finishes, equals) receive positive measure through a tolerance band, and under a single tolerance the thirteen relations form a true partition that recovers crisp Allen as the tolerance vanishes. The construction derives Allen's taxonomy rather than positing it: coarse predicates such as precedence, overlap, and containment are unions of leaves whose probabilities are leaf sums, and this hierarchy is preserved as intervals collapse to points and thirteen relations reduce to five and then three. Each relation further decomposes into correlation-aware temporal primitives in the spirit of CIDOC CRM. The algebra is scale-invariant and separates graded expressions such as "shortly before" from contact relations. All results are Monte-Carlo validated and shipped as an open, tested Python package.

</details>


### [258] [Ownership in AI-Assisted Everyday Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20658)
*Megan Wei,Melanie Subbiah,Audrey Lee,Annya Dahmani,Dave Edwards,Helen Edwards,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过探索性定性调研发现，人对AI辅助成果的"所有权感"取决于协作过程：主导、迭代、改写可保留所有权，而仅批准AI建议则导致疏离；个人声音丧失与理解缺失会削弱所有权，披露意愿则主要受社群规范和信用归属担忧影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI融入日常任务，机器参与创作会如何影响人们的成果所有权感与贡献感，这一问题尚缺乏实证考察。

Method: 开展探索性定性问卷调查，要求参与者描述两件近期自选的AI协作任务：一件感觉属于自己，一件不属于，并进行对比分析。

Result: 所有权感取决于协作过程：仅批准AI建议者会"脱权"，而主导、迭代或改写者保有所有权；当人拥有项目愿景而非执行时所有权仍可延伸，甚至对离开AI无法完成的任务报告高所有权。个人声音丧失与对输出的不理解均侵蚀所有权。披露AI使用的意愿常与真实自豪感或所有权脱钩，由社群规范和"信用被抹除"的担忧塑造。

Conclusion: 基于发现提出若干研究方向，旨在推动支持人们对自身生活拥有作者感的AI发展。

Abstract: When does work done with AI still feel like ours? As AI becomes woven into everyday tasks, we must examine what happens to our sense of ownership and contribution when a machine shares in producing what we make. We report an exploratory qualitative survey in which participants were asked to describe two recent, self-selected tasks completed with AI: one that felt like their own and one that did not. We find that felt ownership depends on the process of collaboration: people disown work when they merely approve AI's suggestions, but retain ownership when they lead, iterate, or rewrite. Ownership can also extend to settings where people own the vision for a project but not the execution; respondents reported high ownership on tasks they could not have completed without AI. Loss of personal voice and a lack of comprehension of the output both erode ownership. Finally, willingness to disclose AI use is often decoupled from actual pride or ownership, and instead shaped by community norms and fear of credit erasure. We propose several research directions as a result of these findings to promote AI development that supports people's sense of authorship over their own lives.

</details>


### [259] [Deep Noir: Autonomous Steering Discovery via Architectural Chronometry in Transformer Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20722)
*Frank E. Bobe,Gregory D. Vetaw,Darshan W. Bryner,Matthew G. Cook,Jose L. Salas-Vernis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Deep Noir框架利用Logit Lens收敛与因果头级归因，自动发现最优激活引导参数，在多规模、多架构上显著提升分类性能并揭示其提示注入攻击面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导能在推理时修改大模型行为，但确定引导位置与强度仍依赖人工。需要自动化发现干预点的方法，以提升通用性并降低手工调参成本。

Method: 提出Deep Noir框架，结合Logit Lens收敛分析与因果头级归因（causal head-level attribution），自主发现最优引导参数；在1B、2-3B、7-9B三档共九个模型（1B×3、2-3B×2、7-9B×4）上评估，任务包括垃圾信息（spam）与SST-2情感分类，并与RepE（无头掩码）对比。

Result: 1B规模垃圾信息任务提升16.7个百分点（标准差4.7；39次运行）；7-9B在四种架构上提升21至42个百分点；SST-2情感任务在零代码改动下提升13.1个百分点。情感任务中RepE无头掩码未能超过基线，而Deep Noir在全部模型上均有提升（p<0.01）。同时发现引导会形成可预测的提示注入攻击面，脆弱性随引导强度单调增加。

Conclusion: 机制性归因使干预点的自动发现可跨任务与架构泛化；引导参数自动化具备实用价值，且其引入的提示注入风险对部署引导分类器的智能体系统具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies LLM behavior at inference time, but identifying where and how strongly to steer remains manual. We introduce Deep Noir, a framework that uses Logit Lens convergence and causal head-level attribution to autonomously discover optimal steering parameters. Across three scales (1B x 3, 2-3B x 2, and 7-9B x 4), our engine achieves 16.7 percentage-point improvement on spam at 1B (standard deviation 4.7; 39 runs), with gains increasing to 21 to 42 percentage points at 7-9B across four architectures. On SST-2 sentiment, it achieves a 13.1 percentage-point improvement with zero code changes. Mechanistic grounding enables automated discovery of intervention points that generalize across tasks and architectures. On sentiment, RepE without head masking fails to improve over baseline, while Deep Noir improves all models (p less than 0.01). We further show that steering creates a predictable prompt-injection attack surface whose vulnerability increases monotonically with steering magnitude. This finding is relevant to agent systems deploying steered classifiers.

</details>


### [260] [An Empirical Study of Harness Design for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20804)
*Run-Ze Fan,Zihao Zhang,Simin Ma,Yebowen Hu,Shouju Wang,Kaiqiang Song,Fei Liu,Hamed Zamani,Xiaoyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过构建一个执行循环固定、仅变动三个组件（规划、动作空间、上下文管理）的轻量级编码框架，对176组匹配设置进行组件级评估，揭示了各组件在不同模型与上下文预算下的差异化作用并给出轨迹级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将编码框架（harness）作为整体系统进行评估，导致其中各组件（如规划、动作空间、上下文管理等）的独立有效性不清晰。作者希望实现组件级比较，明确各组件何时、对何类模型有效，从而指导模型与预算感知的框架设计。

Method: 设计一个轻量级编码框架，固定执行循环，仅变化三个组件：规划、动作空间、上下文管理。在四个模型上，于SWE-Bench Verified与Terminal-Bench 2.1上评估176组匹配设置，覆盖五种上下文管理策略、四种上下文窗口预算，以及对规划与动作空间的针对性消融实验。

Result: (1) 上下文管理在上下文窗口预算收紧时价值上升，主要收益来自防止上下文溢出失败；(2) 先做基于规则的省略再做LLM摘要整体效率最优，而让省略内容可恢复会引入模型很少使用的机制且无精度提升；(3) 规划对弱模型是精度支架，对强模型转为成本节约手段，精度变化不大；(4) 预定义工具提升bash能力较弱模型的表现，而具备bash能力的模型用纯bash接口即可有效工作并在命令行任务上成本显著更低。

Conclusion: 轨迹级分析解释了上述效应：上下文管理延长执行轨迹但不显著改变智能体行为，规划改变轨迹终止位置，动作空间改变代码编写的粒度。这些发现为模型与预算感知的框架设计提供依据，并给出一个评估未来框架组件的模块化框架。

Abstract: Coding harnesses shape how autonomous coding agents translate model capabilities into long-horizon software-engineering performance, yet existing work typically evaluates harnesses as monolithic systems, leaving the effectiveness of individual components unclear. To enable component-level comparisons, we study this question with a lightweight coding harness whose execution loop is fixed while three components are varied: planning, action space, and context management. Across four models evaluated on SWE-Bench Verified and Terminal-Bench 2.1, we evaluate 176 matched settings spanning five context-management strategies, four context-window budgets, and targeted ablations of planning and action space. We find that: (1) Context management becomes increasingly valuable as the context-window budget tightens, with most of its benefit coming from preventing context-overflow failures. (2) Staging rule-based elision before LLM-based summarization provides the strongest overall efficiency among the context-management strategies, whereas making elided content recoverable adds machinery that models rarely use and yields no accuracy gain. (3) Planning shifts from an accuracy scaffold for weaker models to a cost saver for stronger models, with little change in accuracy. (4) Predefined tools improve performance for models with weaker bash proficiency, whereas bash-capable models can operate effectively with a bash-only interface and achieve substantially lower cost, especially on command-line-centric tasks. Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


### [261] [Q&A on Any Spreadsheet Requires Interpreting Its Grid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20732)
*Zofia Smoleń*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于单元格角色标注的电子表格分块框架，提升LLM驱动的RAG系统中分块可解释性；但指出离散分类存在性能天花板，主张转向降维技术将二维非结构化表格直接展平为一维文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子表格是二维非结构化数据，具有连续关系和无限潜在单元格角色；而分类模型只能处理有限预定义类别，即使达到人类级标注也无法完美捕捉结构细节，因此电子表格到LLM的瓶颈需要新的解决思路。

Method: 提出一种新框架：将任意电子表格拆分为可解释分块，通过单元格角色标注（cell role annotation）实现语义单元格注释；并对比现有最先进方法，分析离散分类方法的局限性。

Result: 该框架优于现有最先进方法，但面临硬性天花板；语义单元格注释主要改善分块可解释性，帮助通过丰富上下文生成答案，而非提升检索准确率；离散分类无法完美捕捉表格结构细微差别。

Conclusion: 解决电子表格到LLM的瓶颈需要超越离散单元格分类，转而发展降维技术，将二维非结构化电子表格直接展平为一维非结构化文本，使文本分块更易于下游RAG解释和生成。

Abstract: Semantic cell annotation improves chunking interpretability for spreadsheets in LLM-driven RAG systems, aiding answer generation through enriched context rather than improved retrieval accuracy. We propose a novel framework of splitting any spreadsheet into interpretable chunks using cell role annotation. Our framework beats the state of the art, yet it faces a hard ceiling. Spreadsheets are fundamentally two-dimensional unstructured data with continuous relationships and infinite potential cell roles. Because classification models are restricted to finite, pre-defined classes, they cannot perfectly capture this structural nuance, even with human-level annotation. We show that addressing the spreadsheet-to-LLM bottleneck requires moving beyond discrete cell classification. Instead, the field must develop dimensionality-reduction techniques to directly flatten 2D unstructured spreadsheets into 1D unstructured text. Text chunks would be easier for downstream RAG to interpret and generate from.

</details>


### [262] [RAFT: A Stateful Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20754)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Xiaowen Wang,Anupma Sharan,Zhengyi Chen,Chenyu Diana Zhang,Shanshan Yang,Chittibabu Pacharu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RAFT——一种面向故障排查智能体的有状态 RAG 框架，将历史工单抽象为时间线条目链并进行条目级检索，在合成基准与真实 Jira 数据上均优于 vanilla RAG 与 GraphRAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业客户支持中的故障排查智能体依赖从相似历史案例中检索可执行的解决指导。然而现有 RAG 系统将支持案例视为静态文档，忽略了其多阶段、有状态的本质，导致检索结果无法匹配当前案例所处的中间状态。此外，公开的多阶段故障排查数据极为稀缺，缺乏可直接评估检索层的基准。

Method: 提出 RAFT（Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents）。该框架将每个已关闭的历史案例抽象为一条由时间线条目构成的有向链，并以"条目级"而非文档级进行检索：先匹配活动案例所对应的中间状态，再返回以该匹配状态为锚点的父案例轨迹。另提供可选的案例级图结构，通过可配置的相似度表示在案例之间建立连接。评估聚焦于检索层本身，无需生产环境部署。

Result: 在案例推进的每个阶段，RAFT 的 Case Hit 指标均优于 vanilla RAG 与 GraphRAG 基线，且相对最强基线的提升具有统计显著性。真实 Apache Jira 数据上的结果提供了该优势可迁移至真实案例历史的方向性证据。由于公开的多阶段故障排查数据稀缺，作者构建了基于 Microsoft Learn Windows Server 文档的合成基准，并配对带有真实人工重复标签的 Apache Jira 问题。

Conclusion: 将支持案例建模为有状态的时间线条目链并进行条目级检索，能有效提升故障排查场景下的案例召回质量。作者公开了基准、实现及 Apache Jira 评估集，以缓解该领域多阶段数据稀缺的问题。

Abstract: Effective troubleshooting agents in enterprise customer support depend on retrieving actionable guidance from similar historical cases, yet existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat support cases as static documents and overlook their multi-stage, stateful nature. We introduce RAFT (Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Troubleshooting Agents), a stateful RAG framework that abstracts each closed historical case into a directed chain of timeline entries and retrieves at the entry level, surfacing cases whose intermediate states match the active case and returning the parent-case trajectory anchored at the matched state; an optional case-level graph links cases through a configurable similarity representation. We evaluate this retrieval layer directly, which, unlike evaluating a full agent system, requires no production deployment. Because public multi-stage troubleshooting data is extremely rare, we pair a synthetic benchmark built from Microsoft Learn Windows Server documentation with real Apache Jira issues carrying human-created duplicate labels. RAFT improves Case Hit over vanilla RAG and GraphRAG baselines at every stage of case progress, with statistically significant gains over the strongest baseline; the Jira results provide directional evidence that the advantage transfers to real case histories. We release our benchmark, implementation, and the Apache Jira evaluation set.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个意图驱动的生成式查询建议框架，通过双阶段优化同时提升单条查询质量与整组意图覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议需兼顾两类目标：单条查询对用户有用，且整组建议覆盖不同意图。核心挑战在于如何在生成过程中平衡个体质量与整体意图多样性。

Method: 采用双阶段优化：第一阶段进行意图感知的多样性建模，构建与意图对齐的监督微调（SFT）数据，并引入意图感知多样性奖励来优化意图覆盖；第二阶段进行查询级信用分配，将个体质量信号路由到对应查询的token，同时在整个查询组（slate）层面共享多样性信号。

Result: 在大规模生产数据集上，包括在线A/B测试与离线评估，该方法在点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖度上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 意图驱动的双阶段优化框架有效缓解了生成式查询建议中个体相关性与整体多样性之间的权衡，并在真实场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [264] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分层课程学习（layer-wise curriculum learning）用于高效LLM压缩，通过将模型划分为多层段、结合累积误差理论分析与多线程特征缓存，实现更优压缩性能并显著降低GPU显存与训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型压缩中，知识从教师模型向学生模型迁移存在计算开销大、层间特征错位、训练不稳定及收敛慢等问题，亟需在保证压缩性能的同时提升计算效率、降低显存占用与训练时长。

Method: 1) 采用课程学习策略，由易到难逐步迁移知识；2) 将整个模型划分为由若干层组成的多个段（segment），实现分层（layer-wise）知识迁移以提升计算效率；3) 基于对累积误差现象的理论分析，用分层课程学习加速收敛并稳定知识迁移过程；4) 提出多线程特征缓存方法，解决跨层特征错位问题并最大化GPU利用率。

Result: 在多个数据集上达到state-of-the-art性能；在BERT与GPT-2上GPU显存占用与训练时间均减少50%以上；在相同训练时长下，于LLaMA系列与Qwen模型上以更低GPU显存超越其他剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 分层课程学习能有效兼顾LLM压缩性能与计算效率，在减少显存占用和训练时间的同时取得领先的压缩与剪枝效果，验证了分层课程学习与特征缓存机制在LLM压缩任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [265] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出块并行（BP）与上下文分片块并行（CSBP），利用BDLM目标可按目标块分解的特性，将含噪块计算分配到不同rank并分片共享干净序列，从而突破长上下文训练的通信与显存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型（BDLM）结合块间自回归依赖与块内并行去噪，但长上下文训练受限于分布式注意力通信与激活显存。传统上下文并行（CP）按位置切分“干净+含噪”合并序列，需通信共享的干净K/V以及块特定的含噪K/V及其梯度，开销大。

Method: 观察到BDLM目标在目标块上可分离，提出块并行（BP）：把每个含噪块的计算分配给一个rank。为进一步扩展到长上下文，提出上下文分片块并行（CSBP），在BP基础上将共享干净序列也跨这些rank分片，使含噪K/V与梯度保持本地、避免复制干净前缀，并保持BDLM训练语义不变。

Result: 在16张H200、256K上下文下，CSBP相比最优基线在监督微调上吞吐提升1.18–1.45倍，将自回归模型转换为BDLM提升1.27–1.33倍，同时峰值HBM相当或更低；512K时全模型加速达1.61倍。在8张H100上，512K时DFlash2投机解码器训练加速2.48倍，1M时加速7.59倍。12小时DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT对比中，CSBP在每个训练检查点上于SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench Lite均取得更高通过率。

Conclusion: CSBP通过新的块并行维度有效解决了BDLM长上下文训练中的通信与显存瓶颈，在吞吐、显存及下游任务表现上均优于传统上下文并行，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [266] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种针对二部文本数据的随机化归一化谱共聚类近似方法，分别基于随机投影的随机SVD与部分SVD结合逐元素随机采样，在真实与合成数据集上均较全SVD基线降低运行时间，但效果受矩阵稀疏性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类是挖掘词-文档矩阵潜在结构的有效工具，但其依赖奇异值分解（SVD），标准形式在高维数据上计算代价高昂，因此需要更高效的近似方案。

Method: 针对文档簇与词簇数量可能不同的二部文本数据，提出两种归一化谱共聚类的随机化近似：(1) 通过随机投影实现随机SVD；(2) 将部分SVD与逐元素随机采样相结合。

Result: 在真实与合成数据集上，两种方法均较全SVD基线降低运行时间，但表现取决于矩阵稀疏性：随机投影方法在所测设置中更为可靠；基于采样的方法在较稠密矩阵上最有用，而在本已稀疏的文本数据上收益有限。

Conclusion: 谱共聚类的随机化近似应根据数据底层结构进行选择，而非一概而论。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [267] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），复用无线射频中的混频器在频域被动执行卷积，实现边缘设备上的高效CNN推理，能耗低至0.72 fJ/MAC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能手机、可穿戴设备、无人机等边缘设备上直接运行AI模型具有低延迟、可扩展和数据隐私优势，但这些设备算力难以满足现代神经网络需求；额外的边缘加速器会增加本已受尺寸、重量、功耗和成本（SWaP-C）限制的硬件负担。作者希望利用设备已有硬件——无线射频中的混频器天然在时域做乘法、等价于频域卷积——来替代新增计算硬件。

Method: 提出RF-CNN，将多通道卷积映射到频域音调上，由被动混频器单次完成卷积计算；权重通过无线传输到达，模拟硬件与通信共享，边缘设备仅需承担数据准备和读出能耗。

Result: 实验证明RF-CNN可运行多达2640万参数、9层的深度CNN，覆盖无线信号与图像分类及可控图像生成任务，性能接近全精度；权重空口传输、硬件与通信共享，能耗低至0.72 fJ/MAC，比额外数字处理器低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果表明，已部署的无线基础设施可为它连接的海量设备带来高效、先进的AI推理能力。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [268] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)，刻画循环神经网络在学习过程中由混沌动力学向稳定任务相关动力学的转变，推导出双时关联函数、临界反馈强度与临界时间，并与数值模拟定量吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络的学习会从根本上重塑其底层动力学，将初始的混沌活动转化为稳定的任务相关行为，但缺乏对这一学习过程中动力学转变的理论描述。

Method: 发展非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)来描述学习过程中的转变；证明缓慢的反馈驱动学习过程产生不断演化的有效反馈强度，该强度驱动网络经历由DMFT解的分岔所定义的从混沌到稳定的转变；并在整个学习过程中推导双时关联函数。

Result: 识别出临界反馈强度以及与之对应的、依赖学习率的临界时间，用以区分两种动力学区域；该转变源于不断增长的学习反馈结构对有效动力学势垒的逐步形变；理论从初始未训练状态出发预测了训练期间网络输出的时间演化，并与数值模拟定量一致。

Conclusion: 学习可被理解为由反馈驱动的有效反馈强度演化所引发的DMFT分岔，从而实现从混沌到稳定动力学的转变；该理论为理解学习重塑网络动力学的机制提供了定量框架。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [269] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出个性化联邦分层高斯过程（pFedHGP），在异质客户端间做概率回归与分类，通过“共享全局+客户端偏差+局部残差”三层分解实现个性化联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分布在异质客户端上，传统集中式高斯过程无法建模且隐私受限；联邦场景需要兼顾全局共享信息与客户端个性差异。

Method: 将每个客户端隐函数分解为共享全局成分、共享全局核结构的客户端偏差、灵活局部残差三部分；采用稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断，原始数据不出本地，服务器仅同步共享成分的低维统计量。

Result: 在压机吨位监测中仅用13.77%的标注循环即实现完美故障分类；在联邦空气质量建模中恢复出地理分区，且无需集中化站点级时间序列。另从瞬时线性混合模型视角将分层结构与多输出高斯过程相关联，用于相关传感器建模。

Conclusion: pFedHGP 提供完整的预测分布以支持不确定性决策，在保持数据本地化的同时有效兼顾全局共享与客户端个性化，并可与多输出高斯过程建立理论联系。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [270] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"probe guidance"方法，利用已有扩散模型的冻结内部状态构建引导信号来引导流匹配模型，无需额外前向传播即可实现引导，在连续扩散语言模型上取得无条件生成的最优性能，并揭示了autoguidance的实际机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法（如autoguidance）在推理时需要额外的前向传播，且弱模型与强模型之间动力学的相似性难以保证。此外，autoguidance背后的实际机制目前尚不清楚，亟需深入理解以改进扩散模型。

Method: 提出probe guidance方法：利用现有扩散模型的冻结内部状态（frozen internal states）构造引导信号，其原理与autoguidance类似，但省去了推理时的额外前向传播，并能可靠确保弱模型与强模型具有相似的动力学。将该方法应用于连续扩散语言模型，并利用探针研究强模型为弱检查点这一传统autoguidance设定。

Result: 在连续扩散语言模型上，probe guidance在无条件生成任务上刷新了最先进性能；应用于1.7B扩散语言模型时，在多项选择题问答基准上取得一致提升。针对传统autoguidance设定，发现弱模型必须来自训练的低熵区域。

Conclusion: probe guidance为改进扩散语言模型提供了实用途径，同时揭示了autoguidance背后的实际机制，即弱模型需源自低熵训练区域，从而加深了对该方法的理论理解。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [271] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 无线智能鞋垫结合足底压力与IMU信号，可用机器学习识别坐、站、走及不稳定行走状态；参与者独立验证下HGB宏F1达0.954/0.959（单脚）和0.980（双脚）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒常伴随移动能力、平衡与体位转换的变化，因此需要一种低侵入、可穿戴的传感方案在训练中未见的个体上推断活动状态，为活动监测与跌倒预防提供基础。

Method: 设计无线智能鞋垫平台：每只鞋垫含16个主动压力传感点与六维IMU（三轴加速度+三轴角速度）。采集15名健康成年人数据，采样率80 Hz，切分为重叠窗口。先用分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度与候选模型族，再用参与者独立的5折分层分组交叉验证（同一参与者所有窗口保留在同一折）作为主性能评估。对比模型包括Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting（HGB）与紧凑1D-CNN。

Result: 在参与者独立协议下，HGB的宏F1分别为左脚0.954、右脚0.959，双脚融合达0.980。相同参与者独立折下，紧凑1D-CNN未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，低轮廓鞋类传感可从压力与IMU测量中推断活动状态，并泛化到训练中未见的参与者，为老年照护中的活动监测与跌倒预防奠定基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [272] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将注意力中的 WQ、WK 约束到 Stiefel 流形上，用带信赖域与极分解回缩的 Riemannian Adam 优化；该更新在嵌入度量下为最速下降、尺度无关、良条件且严格 O(d)-等变，并通过五条命题证明权重衰减在 St(d,r) 上黎曼梯度恒为零，从而解释注意力几何得以在衰减驱动的坍缩循环中存留。实验中模算术 grokking 单次运行在 2 万轮达 97.0% 验证精度（基线 61.1%），CIFAR-10 补丁相比 12 组配对起点提升 +8.98pp（t=60.6，12/12），增益随数据增多而扩大；消融表明收益全部来自“步长为自由尺度”，而非投影算子或等变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力中的 query/key 投影通常由欧氏优化器训练，对其几何无任何约束，缺乏几何先验与尺度不变性；作者希望通过对投影施加 Stiefel 流形约束，使注意力学到的几何结构更稳定，并解释其为何能在权重衰减引起的坍缩循环中存活。

Method: 把 WQ、WK 约束在 Stiefel 流形 St(d,r) 上，采用 Riemannian Adam：每帧仅维护一个标量二阶矩，用信赖域限制步长，并以极分解（polar retraction）回缩到流形。理论上给出四条命题，证明该更新是嵌入度量下的最速下降、与梯度尺度无关、良条件、严格 O(d)-等变，均以 float64 数值验证；第五条命题给出机制：权重衰减在 St(d,r) 上的黎曼梯度恒为零（因 W=W I_r 位于法空间），故注意力几何不被衰减破坏。为对照，还分析固定步长黎曼更新（梯度次数为一）与同形 AdamW 矩阵的步长差异。

Result: 模算术 grokking 中单次运行在第 20000 轮取得 97.0% 验证精度，而基线为 61.1%（作者称其为不稳定端点，作为机制证据而非效应量）。CIFAR-10 补丁任务上，同一规则相对 12 组配对起点提升 +8.98pp（t=60.6，12/12 全部获胜），且差距随数据量增加而扩大而非缩小。固定步长黎曼更新每步移动量比同形 AdamW 矩阵小 24–40 倍，其帧几乎不离开初始化，直接冻结这些帧仅损失 0.28pp。消融显示全部增益来自让步长为自由尺度，投影算子与等变性无可测贡献。负结果：不能以“规范消除（gauge removal）”作为方法动机，因为损失不变方向根本不携带梯度。

Conclusion: 在 Stiefel 流形上用 Riemannian Adam 优化注意力投影，可提供尺度自由、良条件的几何约束，使注意力几何在权重衰减下稳定存留；实验增益主要源于步长为自由尺度，而非投影或等变性，权重衰减黎曼梯度恒为零是机制核心。作者强调 grokking 结果为机制证据而非稳健效应量，并提出不能以规范消除作为方法动机的负结果。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [273] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在PARI-HD队列（n=165）中构建泄漏安全的阈值似然朴素贝叶斯分类器，发现I-309/CCL1是认知障碍表格预测中最具增量价值的可解释特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型临床表格数据集需要可解释的机器学习方法：深度学习常不实用，集成模型难以审查。同时存在一个关键误区——统计显著性并不必然意味着预测效用，因此需要在严格的泄漏防护与重复交叉验证框架下评估候选生物标志物的增量预测价值。

Method: 使用PARI-HD队列数据（n=165），实现泄漏安全的阈值似然Bernoulli/Categorical朴素贝叶斯（BNB/CNB）分类器。在每个训练折内，将每个连续预测变量降维为基于监督卡方导出的状态，收入变量通过分类似然进入模型。所有依赖数据的步骤均在重复分层10折交叉验证（30次重复）内完成；采用配对DeLong检验与Benjamini-Hochberg多重校正，并在200个随机划分上评估稳健性。

Result: 人口学基线ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017。I-309（CCL1）为最主要的增量特征，使AUC提升0.110，配对DeLong检验在100%的重复中p<0.05。预设主分析中，I-309的固定划分DeLong p=0.0018，200次随机划分的中位p值为0.0011。在18个候选标志物的探索性家族中，I-309在冻结划分上获得BH校正q=0.032，在85%的随机划分中满足q<0.05，而其他标志物均未表现出可靠的增量预测价值。

Conclusion: 由于所拟合模型是可审查的阈值与类条件概率表，结果表明I-309/CCL1是认知障碍表格预测的可解释候选特征，但仍需外部验证。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [274] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出FCx方法，首次在反事实解释中显式强制可行性，兼顾真实性、低代价与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成真实且低代价的CF，但常忽略可行性，会给出无法构造的修改（如改变种族以获得工作），或与未来变化不兼容。

Method: 基于改进的变分自编码器（VAE），采用多因素损失函数优化；同时支持由领域知识用户指定的硬可行约束和通过因果推断从数据集中自动推断的软可行约束；代价以数值绝对变化（邻近性）和改变特征数量（稀疏性）衡量，真实性以LOF密度估计衡量，确保CF位于密集区域。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上进行了大量实验，结果表明该方法在多个指标上达到最先进水平，同时保证可行性。

Conclusion: FCx首次高效生成同时满足真实性、低代价和可行性的反事实解释，验证了在反事实解释中显式引入可行性约束的有效性。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [275] [Improving Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning via Selective Reward Stimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19414)
*Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RSIQL通过向离线轨迹中具有进展的中间状态引入额外奖励信号，缓解稀疏奖励与长时程目标条件下的价值传播难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习面临稀疏奖励与长时程依赖的挑战：目标完成信息与早期决策在时间上相隔较远，而离线价值估计又会引入额外误差。本文从奖励传播视角研究该问题，并在风格化的延迟目标设定中表明，目标导向的价值分离可能相对于局部估计误差变得很小。

Method: 提出Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning（RSIQL），一种简单的非分层方法。它利用辅助的目标条件价值函数识别被估计为向目标取得进展的中间状态，并在这些状态上施加奖励刺激，从而提供延迟更小的训练监督。与分层方法不同，RSIQL不学习单独的高层子目标策略。

Result: 在D4RL目标到达基准与OGBench上的实验表明，RSIQL平均优于目标条件IQL，并取得与分层离线目标条件方法相当的性能，同时保持简单的扁平策略结构。

Conclusion: 通过在进展中间状态引入奖励刺激以缩短监督延迟，RSIQL在无需分层结构的情况下提升了离线目标条件学习的性能，兼顾了效果与简洁性。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning aims to learn policies that reach specified goals, but remains challenging in offline settings with sparse rewards and long-horizon dependencies. In such settings, goal-completion information can be temporally distant from the early decisions that enable success, while offline value estimation introduces additional error. We study this issue from a reward-propagation perspective and show, in a stylized delayed-goal setting, how goal-directed value separation can become small relative to local estimation error. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Reward Stimulation Implicit Q-Learning (RSIQL), a simple non-hierarchical method that introduces additional reward signals at progress-making intermediate states in offline trajectories. RSIQL uses an auxiliary goal-conditioned value function to identify intermediate states estimated to make progress toward the goal and applies reward stimulation to provide less-delayed training supervision. Unlike hierarchical methods, RSIQL does not learn a separate high-level subgoal policy. Experiments on D4RL goal-reaching benchmarks and OGBench show that RSIQL improves over goal-conditioned IQL on average and achieves performance competitive with hierarchical offline goal-conditioned methods, while retaining a simple flat policy structure.

</details>


### [276] [Bayesian Optimization with Rich Auxiliary Information via LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19437)
*Tejus Gupta,Efe Mert Karagözlü,Rohit Sonker,Barnabás Póczos,Jeff Schnieder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型（LLM）将丰富的辅助信息融入贝叶斯优化（BO），并开发了三种方法，在超参数优化基准和核聚变优化任务上均优于标准BO及现有LLM优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的优化问题往往包含比单纯函数评估值更丰富的信息，例如超参数优化中的训练曲线、科学实验中的专家笔记与图像、关于最优点位置的先验知识。传统BO仅利用函数评估，忽略了这些辅助信息。作者希望探索LLM能否有效利用此类丰富辅助信息来指导优化。

Method: 作者首先验证LLM能够有效利用丰富辅助信息指导优化；在此基础上，提出三种将辅助信息通过LLM融入贝叶斯优化的方法。

Result: 在超参数优化基准测试和一个真实核聚变优化任务中，所提方法一致优于标准贝叶斯优化方法和已有的基于LLM的优化方法。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效利用丰富的辅助信息来改进贝叶斯优化，验证了将LLM与BO结合以处理富信息优化问题的有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely used for optimizing expensive black-box functions, yet many real-world optimization problems contain substantially richer information than function evaluations alone. Examples include training curves in hyperparameter optimization, expert notes and images in scientific experimentation, and prior knowledge about where optima may lie. We show that large language models (LLMs) can effectively leverage such rich auxiliary information to guide optimization. Motivated by these findings, we develop three methods for incorporating auxiliary information into BO using LLMs. Across hyperparameter optimization benchmarks and a real-world nuclear fusion optimization task, our methods consistently outperform both standard BO and existing LLM-based optimization approaches. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLMs for leveraging rich auxiliary information in BO.

</details>


### [277] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对拉曼光谱病原菌耐药性检测中现有算法泛化差、依赖复杂预处理的问题，引入锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器，在临床菌株分类任务中相比Adam最多提升10.5%精度，平均提升2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌素耐药性预计2050年每年致死1000万人，资源有限地区受影响最大。拉曼光谱有望在数小时内实现快速便携的耐药性检测（金标准方法需数天），但现有拉曼光谱分析算法存在两大局限：1）在有限数据集上难以跨多样患者群体泛化；2）因数据质量低，需依赖特征提取等非平凡预处理步骤，导致模型复杂度增加。

Method: 采用锐度感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization, SAM）优化器，以增强模型在临床细菌分离株分类任务中跨多种超参数配置的泛化能力，并与传统优化器Adam进行对比。

Result: 相比Adam，SAM在单一数据划分上精度提升最高达10.5%，在所有划分的平均精度上提升2.7%。

Conclusion: SAM能够提升AI驱动的拉曼光谱工具的泛化性能，有助于推动其在临床中的应用。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods.
