However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [278] [Enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network by Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19466)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Rose Chong-Wu,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出知识增强型农业信息神经网络KAINN，将肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度等机理知识融入神经网络，在多个生长季和不同架构下优于纯数据驱动模型及原AINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业N2O排放预测对环境评估和可持续农业至关重要，但排放受土壤属性、气候、生化过程和管理措施复杂交互影响，且高质量观测数据有限。深度学习虽能捕捉非线性关系，却缺乏物理可解释性，跨环境条件泛化能力差。

Method: 提出KAINN混合神经-机理框架，在农业信息神经网络(AINN)基础上引入关于肥料扩散、土壤呼吸和充水孔隙度的领域知识；采用CNN、LSTM和Transformer三种架构，在多个生长季及多种输入特征配置下进行评估；并分析学习到的接口参数轨迹。

Result: KAINN在RMSE和MAE上普遍低于纯数据驱动模型和原始AINN，R²更高；学习到的接口参数轨迹更平滑、物理一致性更强、不确定性更低。

Conclusion: 将环境知识融入神经网络可提升农业N2O排放预测的可靠性、可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from agriculture is important for assessing environmental impacts and supporting sustainable farming. However, prediction remains difficult because N2O emissions result from complex interactions among soil properties, climate, biochemical processes, and management practices, while high-quality observations are limited. Deep learning models can capture nonlinear relationships but often lack physical interpretability and may generalize poorly across environmental conditions. We propose the Knowledge-enhanced Agriculture-informed Neural Network (KAINN), a hybrid neural-mechanistic framework that extends the Agriculture-informed Neural Network by incorporating domain knowledge about fertilizer diffusion, soil respiration, and water-filled porosity. We evaluate KAINN using CNN, LSTM, and Transformer architectures across multiple growing seasons and input-feature configurations. The results show that KAINN generally provides lower root mean square error and mean absolute error and higher R-squared values than purely data-driven models and the original AINN. Analysis of the learned interfaces also shows smoother and more physically consistent parameter trajectories with reduced uncertainty. These findings demonstrate that incorporating environmental knowledge into neural networks can improve the reliability, interpretability, and generalization of agricultural N2O-emission predictions.

</details>


### [279] [Search at the Cost of Sampling: Nearly-Instant Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19476)
*Donney Fan,Colin Doumont,Aleksandra Kalisz,Paul Duckworth,Jacob R. Gardner,Henry Moss,Geoff Pleiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用线性代理模型与球面隐空间的对称性，为生成式去novo设计中的贝叶斯优化提供近乎闭式解，实现百倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型在去novo发现流程中大规模生成设计，并通过虚拟筛选挑出候选进行实验验证。贝叶斯优化虽能利用历史评估指导后续提议，但其序贯决策的计算开销在虚拟筛选成本较低时成为瓶颈，亟需提高BO效率使其在该场景下可行。

Method: 基于近期关于线性代理模型有效性的工作，构建约束在球面域上的线性模型（高维隐变量在球面上集中），利用球面对称性推导代理建模与采集函数的近乎闭式解，从而大幅降低计算开销。

Result: 方法相较最先进基线取得至少100倍加速，并在分子与图像生成基准上性能持平或更优。

Conclusion: 该方法使贝叶斯优化成为去novo生成流程中可实际即插即用的组件，适用于此前因过慢而无法考虑的场景。

Abstract: Generative models are increasingly central to many de novo discovery pipelines, in which designs are generated at scale and filtered through virtual screens to determine a set of candidates to experimentally validate. While Bayesian optimization (BO) is a natural fit for this setting, as it uses past evaluations to guide future proposals, the computational overhead required for its sequential decision-making becomes a bottleneck when virtual screens are relatively cheap. We make BO practical in this regime by exploiting the unique combination of a linear model constrained to a spherical domain where high-dimensional latents concentrate. We build off recent work justifying the use of linear surrogates, while deriving nearly closed-form solutions to the surrogate modelling and acquisition problems that exploit spherical symmetry. The result is at least a 100x speedup over state-of-the art baselines, with matching or improved performance across molecular and image generation benchmarks. Altogether, our method makes BO a practical drop-in for de novo pipelines where it was previously too slow to consider.

</details>


### [280] [Sample Count Is Not Enough: Candidate-Generation Strategy Shapes the Energy and Performance of LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19499)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多候选测试时扩展中，仅报告候选数 N 不足以描述系统成本；固定 N=8 时，批处理调度比串行调度在能耗和延迟上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时扩展常用候选数 N 描述推理预算，但 N 只反映生成多少候选，不反映生成方式；相同 N 可由单次批处理或多次小批量串行调用完成，其系统成本差异未被现有评估体现。

Method: 使用 Phi-3-mini 与 Qwen2.5-1.5B 在 500 条 GSM8K 提示上，先将 N 从 1 增至 8 测准确率；再固定 N=8，比较 1x8、2x4、4x2、8x1 四种生成调度（a x b 表示 a 次调用、每次 b 个候选），测量延迟、吞吐、GPU-hours 与 GPU 设备总能耗；并在每模型三个独立调度的 A100 节点及短输出 SciQ/V100 实验中验证。

Result: N 从 1 增至 8 时，Phi-3-mini 准确率提升 8.4 个百分点，Qwen2.5-1.5B 提升 18.4 个百分点。固定 N=8 时，在 A100 上八次串行调用（8x1）的 GPU 设备总能耗为单次批处理（1x8）的 4.64–4.86 倍，P95 延迟为 5.77–6.12 倍；该模式在三个独立 A100 节点和 SciQ/V100 短输出实验中一致出现。

Conclusion: 候选数本身不足以刻画多候选测试时扩展的系统成本。当候选相互独立且显存允许时，更少生成调用、更大批大小更高效。评估应同时报告候选数、准确率、生成调度及 GPU 级系统指标。

Abstract: Test-time scaling can improve large language model reasoning by generating and combining multiple candidate responses. In sampling-based methods, the inference budget is often described by the number of generated candidates, N. However, N tells us how many candidates are generated, not how they are executed. The same candidate budget can be produced in one batched generation call or split across several sequential calls with smaller batch sizes. We first study the effect of increasing N on reasoning accuracy using Phi-3-mini and Qwen2.5-1.5B on 500 GSM8K prompts. As expected, increasing N from 1 to 8 improves accuracy by 8.4 percentage points for Phi-3-mini and 18.4 points for Qwen2.5-1.5B. However, accuracy alone does not show the systems cost of using a larger candidate budget. We therefore fix N = 8 and compare four generation schedules: 1x8, 2x4, 4x2, and 8x1, where axb denotes a generation calls with b candidates per call. We measure latency, throughput, GPU-hours, and gross GPU-device energy while keeping the total candidate count fixed. On A100 GPUs, eight serial calls use 4.64-4.86x as much gross GPU-device energy and have 5.77-6.12x the P95 latency of one batched call with eight candidates. The same pattern appears across three independently scheduled A100 nodes per model and in short-output SciQ/V100 experiments. These results show that candidate count alone is not enough to describe the systems cost of multi-candidate test-time scaling. When candidates are independent and memory allows it, fewer generation calls with larger batch sizes are more efficient. Evaluations should therefore report not only candidate count and accuracy, but also generation schedule and GPU-level systems metrics.

</details>


### [281] [LSTM-UT and Recurrent-Depth Transformers on Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19521)
*Aras Kavuncu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了三种循环深度Transformer在跨步骤信息保持机制上的差异，发现有限门控记忆（LSTM-UT）在深度外推与延迟回忆任务上均优于隐藏状态携带型（BUT）和扩展注意力缓存型（CoTFormer）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环深度Transformer重复应用共享计算，但不同架构在多步迭代中如何保持信息存在差异，这一机制差异对模型泛化与检索能力的影响尚缺乏系统比较。

Method: 对比三类结构：仅携带当前隐状态的Block Universal Transformer（BUT）、额外保留扩展注意力缓存的CoTFormer，以及带有限门控记忆的新结构LSTM Universal Transformer（LSTM-UT）。在Rule 30元胞自动机和延迟回忆任务上进行测试，并对状态与缓存进行干预实验。

Result: 在Rule 30上，BUT向未见过循环深度的外推比CoTFormer更可靠，但精度最终仍会下降；干预实验表明CoTFormer的失败源于状态与缓存的交互，纠正当前状态可暂时恢复精度，而保留的历史会削弱该纠正。延迟回忆任务中，BUT虽无直接访问过去状态的能力仍优于CoTFormer，后者无法可靠选取所请求的缓存表示。LSTM-UT在两项任务上均同时提升深度外推与延迟回忆表现。

Conclusion: 有限门控记忆是一种适用于重复计算与后续检索的有效归纳偏置，优于无界注意力缓存与单一隐状态携带方案。

Abstract: Recurrent-depth Transformers apply shared computation repeatedly, but differ in how they retain information across steps. We compare a Block Universal Transformer (BUT), which carries only its current hidden state; CoTFormer, which also retains an expanding attention cache; and a new LSTM Universal Transformer (LSTM-UT) with bounded gated memory. On Rule 30 cellular automata, BUT extrapolates to unseen recurrent depths more reliably than CoTFormer, although its accuracy eventually degrades. State and cache interventions show that CoTFormer's failure depends on their interaction: correcting the current state can temporarily restore accuracy, while retained history can undermine that correction. In a delayed-recall task, BUT also outperforms CoTFormer despite lacking direct access to past states; CoTFormer does not reliably select the requested cached representation. LSTM-UT improves both depth extrapolation and delayed recall over these baselines. The results support bounded gated memory as an effective inductive bias for repeated computation and later retrieval in these tasks.

</details>


### [282] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出压缩活动子空间（CAS）方法，通过结构化等距嵌入先将模型参数映射到压缩空间，再在其中构建活动子空间，大幅降低内存需求，使高维大模型的贝叶斯推断可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准活动子空间方法需存储大量满维度的模型梯度，随着模型规模增大，内存开销变得不可承受，限制了其在高维大模型中的应用。

Method: CAS 首先利用结构化等距嵌入（structured isometric embedding）将模型参数映射到压缩空间，然后在该压缩参数化空间内构建活动子空间，从而在保留对输出影响最大方向的同时减少梯度存储。

Result: 在规模递增的神经网络上验证了 CAS 的可扩展性，同时保持了预测性能与稳健的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CAS 显著降低了活动子空间构建的内存开销，使标准方法难以处理的大模型贝叶斯推断变得可行，兼具可扩展性与预测可靠性。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [283] [FedFIbOS: Fisher Importance based Optimal Submodelling for Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19559)
*Yasmeen Afzal,Jeremiah D. Deng,Haibo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedFIbOS，一种基于 Fisher 信息重要性度量的异构联邦学习子模型选择方法，从理论上为子模型参数选择提供依据，并在多个非 IID 基准上取得优于现有方法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦学习中各客户端计算能力不同，需训练容量受限的子模型。现有方法多采用启发式重要性度量（尤其是参数幅值）选择子模型参数，缺乏收敛性理论支撑；同时部分客户端参与会为 Fisher 分数引入额外估计效应，形成理论空白。

Method: 提出 FedFIbOS：以 Fisher 信息构建子模型掩码误差最小化准则。将子模型选择形式化为 Fisher 加权的二次掩码代理问题，证明在 Fisher 主导排序条件下，raw Fisher top-k 规则可求解该代理问题；Fisher 分数由经验对角 Fisher 信息（平方梯度）高效估计，无需额外优化开销。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100 和 AGNews 上，在 pathological 与 Dirichlet 非 IID 设置下，FedFIbOS 相比当前最优方法精度提升约 10%，且异构性越强提升越显著。

Conclusion: Fisher 信息可作为有理论依据的子模型参数选择准则，FedFIbOS 在保留底层掩码联邦优化收敛结构的同时，实现稳定自适应的参数选择，并显著提升异构联邦学习性能。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated learning requires clients with diverse computational capacities to collaboratively train a global model, where each client trains a capacity-constrained submodel. Existing methods select submodel parameters using heuristic importance measures---most prominently parameter magnitude---without theoretical justification for why these measures support convergence. We identify a fundamental gap: existing parameter selection criteria lack theoretical grounding in the convergence framework, partial client participation introduces additional estimation effects in the Fisher scores. We propose \textbf{FedFIbOS}: Fisher Importance-based Optimal Submodelling for heterogeneous federated learning, using Fisher Information in a principled criterion derived from minimizing submodel masking error. %We formally establish when magnitude selection is equivalent to Fisher selection fail under non-IID heterogeneous federated learning. We theoretically formulate submodel selection through a Fisher-weighted quadratic masking surrogate and show that the raw Fisher top-$k$ rule implemented by FedFIbOS solves this surrogate under a Fisher-dominant ranking condition. The resulting method retains the convergence structure of the underlying masked federated optimization bound. Fisher scores are efficiently estimated from empirical diagonal Fisher information using squared gradients, enabling stable and adaptive parameter selection without additional optimization overhead. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and AGNews under pathological and Dirichlet non-IID settings show FedFIbOS achieves ${\approx}10\%$ higher accuracy than the state of the art, with improvements becoming more pronounced under stronger heterogeneity.

</details>


### [284] [The Complexity Kink: A Prompt-Side Structural Complexity Index for Code-Generation Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19616)
*Michael Hernandez,Tian Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在生成前测量的六维提示词结构复杂度指数，与正确性分离；在5,000条Python提示词、21个模型、105,000次生成上验证其信度（ICC=0.872）并刻画通过率的非单调断点及其对任务类型与构造框架控制的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于生成代码测得的复杂度依赖失败结果：困难提示词可能产出简短但失败的程序，从而被误判为低输出复杂度。因此需要在生成之前、独立于正确性地刻画提示词自身的结构复杂度。

Method: 构建六维提示词侧结构复杂度指数，在生成前打分并与正确性分离。按单评分者初步量表六个区间选取5,000条Python提示词，由4名量表外LLM评分者重新打分，得到19,997行评分（其中4,998条提示词四条评分齐全，复合ICC=0.872）。每提示词评测21个模型，共105,000次生成；采用均值池化分析、任务类型固定效应、构造框架控制、模型特异性拟合及2SLS等识别策略。

Result: 未调整均值池化下，通过率在复合分13.75处出现非单调断点，≤13.75为79.9%，>13.75为87.6%。加入任务类型固定效应后断点移至10.75，区制差距由7.6降至2.1个百分点；构造框架控制将其移至8.50且原始差距为-3.5个百分点，单一框架均无法复现池化的+7.6。模型特异拟合中16个为上升、5个为下降。365条审计清洁扩展在15、16区间与原五模型估计一致，但仅新增14条高于16区间的提示词。在零通过率且Lizard复杂度可计算的生成中，28.5%对应提示词复合分>8而输出复杂度≤10。人类一致性中等且依评分者而异；复述与跨语言重评保持分数排序；过度识别检验拒绝六维联合约束。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一套生成前的测量框架及对可靠性区制的有界观察性分析；由于过度识别检验拒绝联合约束，将复合分视为指数，不对2SLS估计作因果解释。

Abstract: Complexity measured from generated code is failure-dependent: a difficult prompt can yield a short failing program and be assigned low output complexity. We introduce a six-dimension prompt-side structural-complexity index scored before generation and kept separate from correctness. We select 5,000 Python prompts across six bands of a preliminary single-rater rubric. Four out-of-panel LLM raters rescore the locked prompts, giving 19,997 score rows; composite inter-rater reliability is ICC = 0.872 on the 4,998 prompts with all four ratings. We evaluate 21 models per prompt, yielding 105,000 generations. In the unadjusted mean-pooled analysis, pass rate has a nonmonotone breakpoint at composite 13.75, with 79.9% at or below and 87.6% above. This is not a universal failure cutoff. Task-type fixed effects shift the breakpoint to 10.75 and cut the regime gap from 7.6 to 2.1 points. A construction-frame control shifts it to 8.50 with a raw gap of -3.5 points, and neither frame alone reproduces the pooled +7.6-point change. Model-specific fits include 16 upward and five downward changes. A 365-prompt audit-clean extension matches the original five-model estimates at bins 15 and 16 but adds only 14 prompts above bin 16. Among zero-pass generations with computable Lizard complexity, 28.5% pair a prompt composite above 8 with output complexity at most 10. Human agreement is moderate and rater-dependent on a disagreement-enriched calibration set; paraphrase and cross-language rescoring preserve score ordering. Overidentification tests reject the joint restrictions on the six dimensions, so we treat the composite as an index and make no causal interpretation of the 2SLS estimates. The contribution is a pre-generation measurement framework and a bounded observational analysis of reliability regimes.

</details>


### [285] [A Policy Profile for Croissant: Refusal as a Property of the Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19640)
*Alexander Chernov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为 Croissant 数据集描述符补充一套可执行的数据使用条件评估语义：提出加性 profile、五个算子的完整判定过程，并在两套语料与真实 nf-core 流水线部署中验证其决定性、低开销与可剥离性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croissant 1.1 虽通过 schema.org/JSON-LD 承载数据使用条件（推荐 DUO 与 ODRL），但未规定任何评估方式：缺少判定过程、评估成本上界、无法评估条件的处理结果、检查记录机制，以及与调用方权威的组合规则。本文旨在补足这一"被缺失的另一半"——评估语义。

其中 [[GitHub]] 与 [[Croissant]] 是相关平台/规范。

Method: 1) 设计加性 profile：数据集可声明其允许的操作及允许条件，基于封闭的五算子集合；2) 给出五算子的完整判定过程，使 gate 仅凭描述符即可决策并记录所检查内容；3) 引入两套相互独立的评估语料：由真实 nf-core 流水线门控的三个描述符（部署结果），以及由 profile 文法生成的语料（广度覆盖，涵盖所有算子、拒绝类与一致性条款）；4) 将同一策略以 ODRL 写入 usageInfo 作为三方对照。

Result: 1) 部署：profile 文档产出的决策与 gate 原生描述符记录逐条一致；剥离该层后仍为合法 Croissant 文档；附加成本 11.7 μs，对应 119 μs 的决策开销。2) 广度语料覆盖全部算子、拒绝类与一致性条款；其有效用例中 552 条完整决策记录在三方（原生描述符、profile 条款、usageInfo 中的等价 ODRL 策略）一致。3) 调用方绑定策略与数据绑定策略的状态空间不重叠，故任一许可集合都不包含对方——二者不可相互归约。

Conclusion: 贡献不在载体（Croissant/JSON-LD 本身），而在评估语义：通过加性、可剥离的 profile 赋予数据使用条件以决定性、可记录、低开销的判定过程。同时指出调用方权威与数据侧策略在状态空间上互不覆盖，需在组合模型中分别处理而非简单求并或包含。

Abstract: Croissant is the de facto machine-readable descriptor for ML datasets: JSON-LD over schema.org. Since version 1.1 it also carries data use conditions, recommending DUO and ODRL for them. What no version specifies is how any of them is evaluated: no decision procedure, no bound on evaluation cost, no outcome for a condition an implementation cannot evaluate, no record of what was checked, and nothing on composition with caller-side authority. We supply that half. An additive profile lets a dataset declare the operations it admits and the conditions under which it admits them, over a closed set of five operators whose decision procedure is given in full, so a gate decides from the descriptor alone and records what it checked. Two corpora evaluate it and their evidence is kept apart. Three descriptors that gated a real nf-core pipeline give the deployment result: decisions from a profile document match the gate's native descriptor record for record, stripping the layer leaves a valid Croissant document, and the added cost is 11.7 $μ$s against a 119 $μ$s decision. A corpus generated from the profile's grammar gives the breadth, covering every operator, refusal class and conformance clause. Across its valid cases, 552 complete decision records agree three ways -- native descriptor, profile terms, and the same policy as ODRL in usageInfo. The carrier is therefore not the contribution; the evaluation semantics is. Finally, caller-bound and data-bound policies range over non-overlapping state spaces, so neither permit set contains the other.

</details>


### [286] [CoRe: Coherence and Relational Alignment for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19670)
*Xiaoyu Lin,Huiran Duan,Yining Liu,Zhixiang Wu,Chu Lin,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoRe——一种模型无关的直接多变量时间序列预测学习目标，用频域一致性损失和低秩关系图损失替代逐点MSE监督，无额外可训练参数，在多个基准上稳定提升强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接多变量预测虽能一次输出完整预测视野，但训练目标仍多为MSE等逐点误差。此类目标监督稳定，却不显式保留未来轨迹结构：各变量内部的时间连贯性与变量间的关系一致性均可能被削弱。

Method: CoRe用两个输出空间约束替代逐点监督：(1)频域一致性损失，对齐预测谱与真实谱；(2)低秩关系图损失，在由目标导出的PCA子空间中匹配采样得到的成对差分。该目标不引入可训练参数，仅需替换损失即可应用于现有预测骨干网络。

Result: 在标准基准上，CoRe提升了强基线表现，与近期预测目标相比具有竞争力，并在不同骨干、数据集和超参数设置下总体保持一致有效性。

Conclusion: 显式约束输出空间中的时间连贯性与跨变量关系一致性，是改进直接多变量预测的有效且通用的损失设计思路。

Abstract: Direct forecasting has become a standard paradigm for multivariate time-series forecasting because it predicts the full future horizon in a single pass. However, its training objective is often still decomposed into pointwise errors such as MSE. Such objectives provide stable supervision, but they do not explicitly preserve the structure of the future trajectory: temporal coherence within each variable and relational consistency across variables can both be weakened. We propose CoRe, a model-agnostic learning objective for direct multivariate forecasting. CoRe replaces pointwise supervision with two output-space constraints: a frequency coherence loss that aligns predicted and target spectra, and a low-rank relational graph loss that matches sampled pairwise differences in a target-derived PCA subspace. The resulting objective introduces no trainable parameters and can be applied to existing forecasting backbones by changing only the loss. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe improves strong baselines, compares favorably with recent forecasting objectives, and remains effective across different backbones, datasets, and hyperparameter settings overall consistently.

</details>


### [287] [Conservation Buys Stability and Factoring Buys Counterfactuals in Physical World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19674)
*Yufeng Wang,Parivesh Priye,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 学习模拟器在训练条件改变时会出现两类失效：长时程漂移与干预下无法泛化；作者证明二者需要不同的结构补救，并给出可分离的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的模拟器虽能精确复现训练条件，但条件一旦变化就以两种不同方式失效：长时间推演中误差累积导致轨迹偏离物理合理行为；在物理参数被干预时，模型仍沿用训练中见到的规律而非干预后的规律。论文旨在厘清这两种失效的结构成因，并寻找各自对应的、可独立施加的结构性解决方案。

Method: 采用匹配对照实验，逐个移除或替换单一结构组件以实现双重分离。一方面，用辛积分器演化学习到的能量（保持保守动力学的几何结构）；另一方面，通过显式线性分解编码物理耦合（因子化耦合）。对照包括等容量预测器、能量正则化预测器、调优的神经ODE，以及无约束参数化。机制可分离性通过移除负责长时程稳定性的结构、以及移除因子化耦合分别检验，并在三体系统之外及需从像素推断物理状态的设定中验证。

Result: 辛积分器演化学习能量可使推演在最长约100倍训练时程内保持有界且物理有意义，而等容量预测器、能量正则化预测器与调优神经ODE均发散。显式线性分解使模型能遵循从未见过的耦合符号，无约束参数化则锁死在训练规律上。移除负责长时程稳定性的结构不影响反事实迁移；移除因子化耦合则破坏反事实迁移但不消除稳定性。该双重分离在匹配对照下成立，且在三体系统之外和从像素推断状态的设定中依然可见。

Conclusion: 长时程稳定性与规律改变后的泛化源自两种不同的结构承诺，二者可被刻意分别施加而无需依赖对方。这为物理世界模型提供了具体的设计原则：应针对稳定性与反事实泛化分别引入相应的结构归纳偏置。

Abstract: A learned simulator can reproduce its training conditions accurately yet fail in two distinct ways once those conditions change. Over long rollouts, small errors accumulate until the trajectory drifts away from physically plausible behavior; under an intervention on a physical parameter, the model may continue to follow the law seen during training rather than the intervened one. We show that these two failures require different structural remedies. Evolving a learned energy with a symplectic integrator preserves the geometry of the conservative dynamics and keeps rollouts bounded and physically meaningful for up to $100\times$ the training horizon, while equal-capacity predictors, an energy-regularized predictor, and a tuned neural ODE diverge. By contrast, encoding the physical coupling through an explicit linear factorization enables the model to follow a never-seen sign of that coupling, whereas an unrestricted parameterization remains locked to the training law. Crucially, the two mechanisms are separable: removing the structure responsible for long-horizon stability leaves counterfactual transfer intact, while removing the factorized coupling destroys counterfactual transfer without eliminating stability. This double dissociation, established with matched controls that remove or replace one structural component at a time, persists beyond the headline three-body system and remains visible when the physical state must be inferred from pixels rather than provided directly. The result is a concrete design principle for physical world models: long-horizon stability and changed-law generalization arise from distinct structural commitments, and each can be imposed deliberately without requiring the other.

</details>


### [288] [Opinion Dynamics-based Coalition Formation for Federated Learning in Heterogeneous IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19695)
*Mohammed El Hanjri,Anas Abouaomar,Hamidou Tembine,Abdellatif Kobbane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hegselmann-Krause (HK) 有界信任意见动力学模型的联邦学习客户端联盟形成方法，在本地权重空间聚合，用于智能水表短期用水量预测，显著降低MAE并保持与FedAvg相同的通信与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在物联网异构场景（如智慧城市水表网络）中面临统计异质性：各智能水表观测到的是家庭特定的用水时间序列，标准FedAvg将差异较大的本地模型平均为单一全局模型，可能无法捕捉客户端特定模式。

Method: 在本地权重空间中直接形成客户端联盟，并在联盟层级进行聚合。扩展已有的权重驱动联盟形成方案，将联盟形成建模为作用在本地权重上的Hegselmann-Krause (HK) 有界信任意见动力学过程，并基于欧氏距离与余弦相似度两种置信准则开发HK交互变体。采用本地LSTM模型进行短期用水量预测，与FedAvg、Per-FedAvg、FedProx以及基于欧氏距离或余弦相似度联盟形成的FedAvg进行对比。

Result: 在真实智能水表用水数据集上，所提HK联盟形成方法在至多十次内层迭代内产生稳定、内生的联盟结构；相较FedAvg不增加客户端计算或通信开销；平均MAE相对FedAvg最多降低54%，相对FedProx降低39%，相对Per-FedAvg降低24%，同时取得最高的全局精度（83-85%）。

Conclusion: 将HK有界信任意见动力学引入联邦学习的联盟形成，可有效应对客户端统计异质性，在不增加开销的前提下提升预测精度并生成稳定的内生联盟结构。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving, on-device training across heterogeneous Internet-of-Things (IoT) deployments such as smart-city water-metering networks, where each smart meter observes a household-specific consumption time series. Under such statistical heterogeneity, the standard Federated Averaging (FedAvg) aggregation averages dissimilar local models into a single global model that may fail to capture client-specific patterns. We address this by forming client coalitions directly in the local-weight space and aggregating at the coalition level. Extending a prior weight-driven coalition-formation scheme, we model coalition formation as a Hegselmann-Krause (HK) bounded-confidence opinion-dynamics process acting on the local weights, and develop variants of the HK interaction based on Euclidean-distance and cosine-similarity confidence criteria. The framework is applied to short-term water-consumption forecasting with local Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) models and evaluated against FedAvg, Per-FedAvg, FedProx, and FedAvg with Euclidean-distance or cosine-similarity coalition formation. Experiments on a real smart-metering dataset of water consumption show that the proposed HK-based coalition formation produces stable, endogenous coalition structures within at most ten inner iterations, incurs no additional client-side computation or communication compared to FedAvg, and reduces the average MAE by up to 54% relative to FedAvg, 39% relative to FedProx, and 24% relative to Per-FedAvg, while achieving the highest global accuracy (83-85%).

</details>


### [289] [Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling: A Specification and Design Guide for Contrast-Based Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19709)
*Sulgi Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling (OR-TS)，在分批更新的 bandit 中携带对数几率对比的联合后验，并在每批次重新拟合（边际化）公共水平项，以替代传统的绝对奖励率记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分批更新的 bandit 通常把每条臂的绝对奖励率从一次更新带到下一次。当共享的水平项在批次间漂移时，这种记忆会失效，即使臂间比较关系并未改变。因此需要一种只保留相对对比、对水平漂移稳健的更新方式。

Method: OR-TS 在贝叶斯 bandit 智能体中携带对数几率对比的联合后验，每个批次重新拟合公共水平并对其边际化；智能体包含两个控制参数：decay（过去证据在更新后保留的程度）与 aggressiveness（信念转化为分配的锐度）。与绝对率记忆进行对比评估。

Result: 在 86 个公开 A/B 序列中，水平项的变动约为对比项的 25 倍。在预设合成环境中，水平漂移使绝对率记忆的 regret 增至 5 倍，且 20 次运行中有 7 次最优臂流量不足一半，而 OR-TS 为 0 次。在 71 个真实实验的策略仿真中，对比过小难以分辨时，58 个的期望点击差异在 0.1% 以内，但对比记忆仍以两倍以上频率落在更优臂上。当对比本身发生移动时，该方法失效，论文亦报告了该情形。

Conclusion: 在共享水平项显著漂移、臂间对比相对稳定的分批 bandit 场景中，携带对比后验而非绝对奖励率记忆能显著降低 regret 并更可靠地锁定最优臂；但该方法依赖对比稳定这一假设，当对比自身移动时会失败。

Abstract: Batched multi-armed bandits update on a service's own schedule, and the usual implementation carries each arm's absolute reward rate from one update to the next. When the shared level moves between batches, that memory goes stale even though the comparisons between arms may not have. Odds-Ratio Thompson Sampling (OR-TS) instead carries the joint posterior over log-odds contrasts and fits the common level afresh in every batch, marginalizing it out. This paper specifies that update, places it inside a Bayesian bandit agent with two controls, decay for how much past evidence survives an update and aggressiveness for how sharply belief becomes allocation, and evaluates it against absolute-rate memory. Across 86 public A/B series the level varies about twenty-five times more than the contrast. In prespecified synthetic environments a moving level costs absolute-rate memory five times the regret and leaves the best arm below a majority of traffic in 7 of 20 runs, against none for OR-TS. In a policy simulation built from 71 real experiments, where the contrasts are too small to resolve, expected-click differences stay within 0.1% for 58 of them, yet contrast memory still ends on the better arm more than twice as often. Where the contrasts themselves move, the bet fails, and that case is reported too.

</details>


### [290] [Learn Your Own Thoughts: Abstract Token Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19717)
*Khashayar Gatmiry,Avrajit Ghosh,Parsa Mirtaheri,Jason D. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Emmanuel Abbe,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Abstract Token Curriculum (ATC) 课程学习框架，无需显式监督或人工设计草稿纸，即可通过连续表征空间逐步培养模型的内部抽象"思维"，并在理论与实验上验证其优于现有连续思维训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT 技术虽能提升 LLM 推理能力，但依赖对思维 token 的显式监督，需要大量任务特定的标注数据，成本高且难以泛化。作者希望在不依赖直接监督和人工设计 scratchpad 的前提下，让模型自发形成有效的连续中间表征。

Method: ATC 框架：通过一系列逐渐增加问题复杂度的数据分布构成课程，循序渐进地训练模型，使其在连续表征空间中发展出内部抽象"思维"。该方法不直接监督思维 token，也不需人工设计草稿步骤。

Result: 理论层面：证明在单层 softmax 注意力模型上学习奇偶校验函数时，ATC 使注意力自然聚焦于上下文中提供"最易路径"的 CoT token，以预测下一个 token。实验层面：在**图可达性**与**算术学习**任务上验证了 ATC 的有效性。

Conclusion: ATC 提供了一种无需显式监督即可诱导连续隐式思维的有效课程学习方案，兼具理论保证与实验支持，较以往连续思维训练方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable reasoning capabilities by utilizing chain-of-thought (CoT) as a scratchpad for intermediate stages of thinking. However, CoT techniques require explicit supervision on thinking tokens, which requires rich, task-specific data. In this work, we propose Abstract Token Curriculum (ATC), a novel curriculum learning framework that elicits effective continuous intermediate representations without direct supervision or manual scratchpad design. ATC gradually increases problem complexity through a sequence of distributions, training the model to develop internal abstract ``thoughts'' in the continuous representation space. This paper provides both theoretical and experimental evidence for the benefits of ATC and its advantages over previous methods for training continuous thoughts. Theoretically, we show that for learning parity functions with single-layer softmax attention using ATC, attention naturally focuses on the CoT tokens in the context that provide the ``easiest path'' to predicting the next token. Experimentally, we show ATC's effectiveness on graph reachability and arithmetic learning tasks.

</details>


### [291] [Alliance Beats Isolation: Unifying Heterogeneous Allied Datasets Improves Classifier Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19748)
*Girish Keshav Palshikar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出将两两"联盟异构数据集"的特征空间合并为统一特征空间，并用矩阵补全构建统一数据集，从而在多个分类器上持续超越分别训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中（如学生辍学、保险欺诈、贷款审批、机器故障）存在大量公开标注数据集：它们针对同类对象但底层样本互不相交、类别标签相同、特征空间高度异构且仅有少量共享特征。这类"联盟数据集"无法直接合并训练单一分类器，也难以跨数据集测试，导致知识无法共享，性能受限。

Method: 首先将一对联盟异构数据集的特征空间合并（merge）为单一统一特征空间；随后采用矩阵补全（matrix completion）方法在该合并特征空间上构建统一数据集，以填补缺失的特征—样本项；其核心假设是统一表示可促进分类知识从一个数据集迁移到另一个数据集。

Result: 在多对联盟异构数据集及多种分类器上进行实验，结果显示：在统一表示上训练的任何分类器，其性能始终优于在各联盟数据集上分别训练的分类器。

Conclusion: 通过统一并联合使用多个联盟数据集，可提供一种简便途径显著提升分类器性能。

Abstract: In many application domains, such as student dropout, insurance fraud, loan approval, and machine failures, several labelled public datasets are available where (i) data is about the same type of objects but the set of actual underlying objects are disjoint; and (ii) the class labels are same; and (iii) the feature spaces of the datasets are largely distinct (heterogeneous), with a few shared features. We call such datasets as allied. A single classifier cannot be trained on both datasets together, and one classifier trained on one dataset cannot be tested on the other. In this paper, we propose a method to merge the feature-spaces into a single feature-space for a pair of given allied heterogeneous datasets. We then use a matrix completion method to create a unified dataset based on the merged feature-space. The hypothesis is that the merged representation facilitates the transfer of classification knowledge from one dataset to another. We conduct experiments on several pairs of allied, heterogeneous datasets and several classifiers to demonstrate that any classifier trained on the unified representation always outperforms classifiers separately trained on the constituent allied datasets on several pairs of allied datasets. This work provides an easy way to substantially improve classifier performance by unifying and using multiple allied datasets together.

</details>


### [292] [OceanMoE: Structured Conditional Sparse Computation for Long-Horizon Multivariate Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19768)
*Yishun Zhu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OceanMoE，一种结构化条件稀疏专家混合框架，用于多变量海洋预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量海洋预测需兼顾耦合系统中的共享演化与不同预测变量、位置上的异质统计与动力学特征；完全共享模型缺乏灵活性，完全独立模型则丢弃跨变量的海洋共性上下文，因此需在统一模型中保留共享上下文的同时允许按预测目标和局部状态进行专门化计算。

Method: OceanMoE融合跨变量信息构建目标特定的局部表征，并在每个空间位置进行内容条件稀疏路由，活跃专家数量随路由器置信度自适应调整；解码器中路由引入由球谐空间基参数化的可学习地理偏置，同时通过共享残差通路与季节通路提供跨变量与月依赖的公共上下文。

Result: 在长时程自回归ORAS5预测实验中，OceanMoE在两种评估设置下均降低总体预测误差，并在大多数后期滚动月份保持低于对应基线的几何平均归一化RMSE；路由分析表明专家分配随预测目标与空间位置变化。

Conclusion: 结果表明结构化条件计算可作为平衡共享海洋上下文与自适应专门化的建模策略。

Abstract: Multivariate ocean forecasting must exploit shared evolution in a coupled ocean system while adapting to the heterogeneous statistical and dynamical characteristics of different prediction variables and locations. Fully shared models may lack the flexibility to handle this heterogeneity, whereas fully independent models discard the common ocean context shared across variables. The key question is how to retain shared context in a unified model while allowing computation to specialize according to the prediction target and local state. We propose OceanMoE, a structured conditional sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework that combines sharing and specialization for multivariate ocean forecasting. OceanMoE fuses cross-variable information to construct target-specific local representations and uses them to perform content-conditioned sparse routing at each spatial location, with the number of active experts adapted to router confidence. In the decoder, routing is augmented with a learned geographic bias parameterized by spherical-harmonic spatial bases, while shared residual and seasonal pathways provide common cross-variable and month-dependent context. Experiments on long-horizon autoregressive ORAS5 forecasting show that OceanMoE lowers aggregate forecasting error in both evaluated settings and maintains lower geometric-mean normalized RMSE than the corresponding baselines over most later rollout months. Routing analyses further show that expert allocation varies with prediction targets and spatial locations. These results support structured conditional computation as a modeling strategy for balancing shared ocean context with adaptive specialization.

</details>


### [293] [PhyRestore: Physics-Structured Latent-Factor Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19776)
*Ahmed Shafee,Chayan Lahiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PhyRestore——一个物理结构化的潜因子修复框架，通过恢复受损的 RUSLE 物理因子并依据已知物理关系重建土壤流失时序变化，在流域尺度双时相栅格上与退化 RUSLE 及多种机器学习模型进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土壤流失时序变化估计中，具有物理意义的输入因子常含噪声或损坏；且显著变化相对大量几乎不变的位置而言十分稀少，导致直接预测变化或修正退化物理估计都很困难。

Method: 基于修正通用土壤流失方程（RUSLE），不直接预测土壤流失变化，也不修正退化的物理估计，而是修复受损的物理因子，再通过已知物理关系重建时序变化。在流域尺度双时相栅格设置下，分别考虑降雨侵蚀力和覆盖管理因子的单独损坏与同时损坏，并与退化 RUSLE 估计及 Direct RF、XGBoost、MLP、CNN 对比。

Result: 当受损因子仍可辨识时，因子修复能改善高幅值变化的恢复效果；但在因子联合损坏、正极端值稀疏以及因子值超出训练支撑域的情况下，其优势减弱。

Conclusion: 物理结构化的潜因子修复在因子可辨识条件下有助于高幅值土壤流失变化恢复，但其收益受联合损坏、极端稀疏与分布外因子值限制，适用性存在边界。

Abstract: Estimating temporal soil-loss change is challenging when physically meaningful input factors are noisy or corrupted, particularly because substantial changes are rare relative to the large number of locations exhibiting little change. We study this problem through the Revised Universal Soil Loss Equation (RUSLE) and introduce PhyRestore, a physics-structured latent-factor restoration framework. Rather than directly predicting soil-loss change or correcting a degraded physical estimate, PhyRestore restores corrupted physical factors and reconstructs temporal change through the known physical relationship. We evaluate PhyRestore in a watershed-scale bitemporal raster setting under isolated and simultaneous corruption of rainfall erosivity and cover management, comparing it with the degraded RUSLE estimate and Direct RF, XGBoost, MLP, and CNN models. Factor restoration improves high-magnitude recovery when the corrupted factors remain identifiable, but its advantage weakens under joint corruption, sparse positive extremes, and factor values outside the training support.

</details>


### [294] [Learning-Based Reconstruction of Optical Properties in Bilayered Media from Single-distance Time-Resolved Reflectance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19786)
*Caterina Amendola,Giulia Maffeis,Lorenzo Buffoni,Lorenzo Chicchi,Francesco Coghi,Duccio Fanelli,Raffaele Marino,Fabrizio Martelli,Riccardo Paoli,Lorenzo Pattelli,Lorenzo Spinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出机器学习框架从时域反射测量重建双层介质的光学属性，精度与速度均优于传统基于模型的反演算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于扩散方程的反演方法在层状生物介质中难以处理结构异质性，对浅层吸收与深层散射的估计精度较差，且需关于层数的先验信息。

Method: 利用精确蒙特卡洛模拟在多源-探测器距离下生成稳健的前向DTOF合成数据集，训练机器学习流水线，并与先进模型反演方法进行基准对比。

Result: 机器学习方法显著提升重建速度，并在精度上超过基于模型的反演求解器，同时无需层数先验即可估计参数空间维度。

Conclusion: 机器学习为层状介质光学属性重建提供了高效且精确的替代方案；未来可通过联合多距离、多DTOF曲线的重建策略进一步提升精度。

Abstract: The inverse problem of reconstructing optical properties, specifically absorption and scattering coefficients, in layered biological media from time-domain reflectance measurements remains a significant challenge for traditional analytical models. Inverse solvers based on the diffusion equation often struggle with structural heterogeneity, frequently yielding poor accuracy for superficial absorption and deep-layers scattering. In this work, we propose a machine learning framework as an alternative approach to reconstruct the optical properties of a bilayered medium, benchmarking its efficiency and accuracy against model-based algorithms. To overcome the intrinsic approximations of diffusion theory and inverse reconstruction, we generated a robust synthetic dataset of forward DTOF using exact Monte Carlo simulations at multiple source-detector distances. A machine learning pipeline was then trained on this dataset and validated against state-of-the-art model-based reconstruction methods. Besides the significant reconstruction speed-up, the machine learning approach achieves higher accuracy than model-based inverse solvers, further providing an estimate of the parameter space dimensionality without requiring any a priori information about the number of layers in the investigated geometry. Further enhancements in the reconstruction accuracy can be expected in future extensions of this work, by training the pipeline over multiple DTOF curves from the same medium, in a joint multi-distance reconstruction approach.

</details>


### [295] [DeliveryGym: An RL Environment for Long-Horizon Embodied Agent Planning with Adaptive Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19801)
*Haoqiang Kang,Yiming Zhang,Yiyang Guo,Chuying Li,Jianzhi Shen,Tianruo Rose Xu,Xiaokang Ye,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DeliveryGym——3D连续外卖配送班次环境，耦合多模态工具交互与持久世界动态，用轨迹级奖励训练/评测LLM具身智能体的规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体的行为后果不止当前任务成败，还消耗后续任务所需的时间、能量与金钱，因此需要能保留这些依赖并转化为完整轨迹反馈的可执行环境，以支持强化学习。

Method: 构建DeliveryGym 3D环境，模拟连续配送班次：结合多模态工具交互与持久世界动态；奖励由仿真事件计算得到，可提供轨迹级RL信号；训练时按策略暴露的弱点自适应调整后续班次，评测集保持固定。

Result: 在6个模型与13张城市地图上评测，揭示"可靠完成指派配送"与"整班次选单与排序"之间存在明显能力差距。固定测试集上RL使Qwen3-VL-4B净收入提升54.3%；相同rollout预算下自适应训练环境比均匀采样再提升16.5%测试收入。

Conclusion: DeliveryGym提供了研究智能体如何在单个episode内协调配送、为后续订单保留资源的可执行实验平台；结果表明从完整班次学习及"练什么情境"同样重要。

Abstract: Executable environments enable LLM agents to learn from the consequences of their actions. For embodied agents, those consequences extend beyond whether the current task succeeds: completing a delivery can consume the time, energy, or money needed for later work. Learning to plan therefore requires environments that preserve these dependencies and turn them into feedback across a complete trajectory. We introduce DeliveryGym, a 3D environment for evaluating and training agents on continuous courier shifts. It couples multimodal tool interaction with persistent world dynamics and computes trajectory rewards from simulator events, making the costs of an agent's decisions available for reinforcement learning (RL). The environment also adapts future training shifts to the policy's observed weaknesses while keeping evaluation fixed. Across six models and 13 city maps, evaluation exposes a gap between reliably executing assigned deliveries and choosing and sequencing work over a shift. On the fixed test suite, RL improves Qwen3-VL-4B's net income by 54.3%, showing that learning from complete shifts improves performance under these coupled constraints. Adapting the training environment improves test income by 16.5% over uniform sampling at the same rollout budget, indicating that which situations an agent practices also matters. DeliveryGym provides an executable setting for studying how agents learn to coordinate deliveries and preserve resources for later orders within an episode.

</details>


### [296] [Beyond Flattened Tokens: Structure-Preserving EEG Decoding with Reusable TriDim Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19842)
*Shiyue Su,Song Wang,Zekai Zhan,Junjie Zeng,Ziling Lu,Zongsheng Li,Xinyuan Ye,Zhiyuan Ma,Xinke Shen,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可复用模块 TriDim，将EEG表示为通道、patch内采样点、patch位置三轴显式结构，并组合出独立解码器TriDimEEG，在跨被试评测中取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG解码架构多依赖分离的专用模块，或把多类结构压缩为单一token序列，难以在主干网络中保持通道组织、局部波形动态与长程时序上下文各自的作用并协调其交互。

Method: TriDim块保留三轴显式表示形状：通道（空间）、patch内采样位置（短期时序）、跨记录patch位置（长期时序）。每块沿各轴做前馈变换，并通过跨轴注意力实现轴间信息交换；堆叠TriDim块并配合多层级三轴读出，构建独立EEG解码器TriDimEEG。

Result: 在涵盖临床诊断、睡眠分期、运动想象与情绪识别的八个数据集上，采用严格跨被试评估，TriDimEEG在15个对比模型中总体性能最佳，平均准确率相对次优模型提升4.3%。将三个EEG基础模型中的Transformer块替换为TriDim块，下游准确率平均相对提升7.4%，参数量减少17.0%–47.3%。

Conclusion: TriDim是一种有效且可复用的EEG建模块，TriDimEEG是性能强劲的独立EEG解码器，代码与参数已开源。

Abstract: Effective EEG decoding requires representations that preserve organization among channels, local waveform dynamics, and long-range temporal context. Existing EEG architectures often capture these structures using separate specialized modules or collapse them into a single token sequence, making it difficult to maintain their distinct roles and coordinate their interactions throughout the backbone. We propose TriDim, a reusable block that preserves the representation shape and keeps three EEG axes explicit: channel, sample position within each patch, and patch position across the recording. These axes correspond to spatial, short-term temporal, and long-term temporal information, respectively. Each TriDim block applies feed-forward transformations along individual axes and cross-axis attention to coordinate information exchange among them. By stacking TriDim blocks with a multi-level tri-axis readout, we construct TriDimEEG, a standalone EEG decoder. Under strict cross-subject evaluation on eight datasets spanning clinical diagnosis, sleep staging, motor imagery, and emotion recognition, TriDimEEG achieves the best overall performance among fifteen evaluated models, with a 4.3% relative improvement in average accuracy over the second-best model. Replacing Transformer blocks in three EEG foundation models with TriDim blocks yields an average relative improvement of 7.4% in downstream accuracy while reducing parameter counts by 17.0% to 47.3%. These results establish TriDim as an effective and reusable building block and TriDimEEG as a strong standalone EEG decoder. Code and parameters of TriDimEEG are available at https://github.com/ncclab-sustech/TriDim_model.

</details>


### [297] [Expected Hypervolume Maximization for Multiobjective Optimization under Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19858)
*Victor Trappler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将不确定性下的多目标优化建模为贝叶斯决策问题，以超体积的期望值（EHVI）作为优化目标，提出基于梯度的随机优化方法，并利用高斯过程作为可微代理模型，配合主动学习采集函数，在简单解析问题上验证了策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统不确定性多目标优化通常对每个目标取期望，忽略了不确定性对整体解集质量的影响，缺乏对决策风险的合理刻画。作者希望以贝叶斯决策视角处理该问题，直接优化解集在不确定性下的期望超体积。

Method: 将问题表述为贝叶斯决策问题，最大化有限输入点集合上的期望超体积（EHVI）；在随机优化框架中使用基于梯度的优化方法，并特别处理被支配点的问题；当缺少可微代码时，采用高斯过程作为可微代理模型；同时提出若干基于采集函数的主动学习策略以构建适配该多目标问题的代理模型。

Result: 在若干简单解析问题上比较了所提主动学习策略的性能，验证了方法的可行性与不同策略间的效果差异。

Conclusion: 以期望超体积最大化为目标的贝叶斯决策框架结合梯度随机优化与高斯过程代理模型，是处理不确定性多目标优化的有效途径，且合适的主动学习采集函数能显著改善代理模型质量。

Abstract: The problem of multiobjective optimization under uncertainties is often approached by taking the expectation of each objective. In this work, we propose instead to formulate this as a Bayesian decision problem and to rely on the expected value of the hypervolume, which is to be maximized with respect to a finite set of input points. We show that this can be performed using methods based on gradients in a stochastic optimization framework, provided that care is taken with respect to dominated points. Moreover, in the absence of readily available differentiable code, we propose to use Gaussian Processes as differentiable surrogate models, in order to perform the optimization. An additional contribution in this work are some active learning strategies, through acquisition functions which helps construct a surrogate model well-designed for the multiobjective optimization problem at stake. These strategies are compared on simple analytical problems to assess their performances.

</details>


### [298] [Pretrained Medical Representations for the Practical Screening of Drug Repositioning Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19865)
*Yuhei Fujioka,Daitaro Misawa,Shingo Fukuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一预训练框架，融合层次子词聚合、部分掩码与交叉引用机制，提升医学代码序列表征学习，并构建面向阿尔茨海默病的假设生成与排序筛选流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于BERT的模型难以充分捕捉医疗代码的层次结构及诊断与治疗间的复杂交互，限制了从表征学习向科学假设发现的拓展。

Method: 提出统一预训练框架，显式整合层次化子词聚合、部分掩码和交叉引用机制；在假设排序阶段引入任务自适应表征方法，缓解诊断向量对历史处方信息的过度编码。

Result: 在预训练目标与下游临床事件预测任务（痴呆发病、住院）上持续优于现有方法；在针对阿尔茨海默病的计算机药物重定位案例中，无需依赖文献等外部知识即数据驱动地重现了已知有前景的药物，并实现稳健的假设优先级排序。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了基于观察性关联的假设生成与排序探索性筛选流程；框架不提供因果证据，旨在筛选候选以供后续严格因果推断，表明领域知识驱动的表征学习结合任务自适应表征控制可支撑实用的假设发现工作流。

Abstract: Representation learning from medical code sequences in electronic health records and medical claims data has been successful in various clinical applications, such as those regarding disease prediction. However, significant challenges remain in extending this approach to the discovery of scientific hypotheses. One reason is that many existing BERT-based models fail to adequately capture the hierarchical structure of medical codes and the complex interactions between diagnoses and treatments. To address these limitations, we propose a new unified pre-training framework that explicitly integrates hierarchical sub-token aggregation, partial masking, and cross-reference mechanisms. The proposed model consistently outperformed existing methods on both pre-training objectives and downstream clinical event prediction tasks, including the onset of dementia and hospitalization. We also conducted an in silico drug repositioning case study targeting Alzheimer's disease. In the hypothesis generation step, our approach successfully rediscovered known promising drugs in a data-driven manner without relying on such external knowledge sources as the literature. Subsequently, in the hypothesis prioritization step, we introduced a Task-Adaptive Representation Approach to alleviate the over-encoding of historical prescription information within diagnostic vectors, enabling the robust prioritization of generated hypotheses. This study establishes an exploratory screening workflow for hypothesis generation and prioritization based on observational associations. Importantly, this framework is not intended to provide causal evidence, but rather to identify promising candidates for subsequent rigorous causal inference. Overall, this study demonstrates that domain-informed representation learning combined with task-adaptive representation control can enable a practical hypothesis discovery workflow.

</details>


### [299] [AURA: Adaptive Uncertainty-Routed Analysis for Email Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19873)
*Omran Berjawi,Walid fahs,Rida Khatoun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态邮件威胁检测系统 AURA，利用 URL 分类器的不确定性路由，仅对模糊样本进行语义分析，在分布内与真实分布漂移场景下均取得高 F1 表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邮件垃圾与钓鱼攻击仍是关键安全威胁；攻击者借助大语言模型生成语境上更具说服力的恶意邮件，现有检测系统难以跟上其演化节奏，跨多样且不断变化的攻击场景的泛化能力有限，导致实际部署后效果下降。

Method: AURA 为多模态邮件威胁检测系统，同时分析邮件正文内容与内嵌 URL。系统采用两层结构：第一层由 URL 分类器量化预测不确定性，仅将判定模糊的邮件升级至经过微调的 Transformer 编码器进行语义分析。

Result: 在八个异构训练语料及两个跨十年对抗活动的留出真实语料上评测：分布内宏平均 F1 达 0.9858；在 NazPhish-Eval 与 GuenterTrap-Eval 上分别为 0.9502 与 0.9436。

Conclusion: 不确定性路由的多模态架构可在真实分布漂移下保持稳健泛化能力，为应对演化中的邮件威胁提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Email spam and phishing attacks remain a critical security threat. Adversaries increasingly exploit large language models to craft contextually convincing malicious messages, and existing spam detection systems often struggle to keep pace. Generalization across diverse and evolving attack scenarios is limited, which reduces effectiveness once these systems are deployed in practice. This paper introduces Adaptive Uncertainty-Routed Analysis (AURA), a multimodal email threat detection system that analyzes both the content of an email and its embedded URLs. AURA is built around two layers: the first quantifies prediction uncertainty from a URL classifier, and only ambiguous messages are escalated to a fine-tuned transformer encoder for semantic analysis. The system is evaluated on eight heterogeneous training corpora together with two held-out real-world corpora spanning a decade of adversarial campaigns. AURA reaches a macro F1-score of 0.9858 in-distribution, and on NazPhish-Eval and GuenterTrap-Eval it maintains 0.9502 and 0.9436, respectively, which is evidence of robust generalization under genuine distribution shift.

</details>


### [300] [Uni-LaDiR: Latent Diffusion Unifies Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19878)
*Haoqiang Kang,Yizhe Zhang,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Yian Ma,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Uni-LaDiR提出统一潜在扩散推理框架，将多模态思维映射到共享潜在空间，用扩散模型预测思维块，在VLM和VLA任务上取得显著增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推理方法通常将各模态的思维token拼接在单一序列中，导致模型需自行弥合模态间的表示差异，推理效率与效果受限。

Method: 引入统一编码器，将来自不同模态的教师推理步骤映射为共享思维token，并训练其保留后续推理步骤及最终答案/动作所需信息；采用扩散模型从输入及前序思维块预测下一思维块；编码器与扩散推理器共享权重联合训练，使思维token既对任务有用又可从上下文预测；推理时无教师观测即可生成token。

Result: 在11个VLM基准和2个VLA套件上，相对最强基线分别取得7.3%（视觉推理）和6.1%（机器人操作）的相对提升。

Conclusion: 将多模态思维统一到共享潜在空间并结合扩散预测，可有效提升跨模态推理与机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires models to draw on information from multiple modalities throughout the reasoning process. Yet existing methods often concatenate modality-specific thought tokens in a single sequence, leaving the model to bridge representational differences as it reasons across modalities. We introduce Uni-LaDiR (Unified Latent Diffusion Reasoner), a framework that brings these thoughts into a shared latent space for reasoning. A unified encoder maps teacher reasoning steps from different modalities into shared thought tokens, trained to preserve the information needed for later reasoning steps and the final answer or action. Because the same context can support multiple valid next steps, we use diffusion to predict the next block of thought tokens from the input and preceding blocks. Jointly training the encoder and diffusion reasoner with shared model weights encourages thought tokens to be both useful for the task and predictable from the available context. At inference, the model generates these tokens without teacher observations. Across eleven vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks and two vision-language-action (VLA) suites, Uni-LaDiR achieves relative gains over the strongest evaluated baselines of 7.3% on visual reasoning tasks and 6.1% on robot manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [301] [Online Adaptive Kernel Mixing for Gaussian Process Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19891)
*Kavin Aravindan,Mani Tej Sriram,Gautam Dasarathy,Tejas Bodas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HACK GPs，将核选择建模为在线专家建议学习问题，用AdaHedge在线更新候选核的权重分布，提供MoG与类别采样两种变体，并给出权重集中与采集函数接近最优专家的理论保证，在BO、LSE、BAL上优于标准核与简单集成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程（GP）广泛用作贝叶斯优化（BO）、水平集估计（LSE）和贝叶斯主动学习（BAL）中黑盒函数的代理模型，其性能关键取决于核函数。当核设定错误（misspecification）时，标准核会导致次优决策，因此需要一种能够自适应选择核的方法。

Method: 将核选择视为带专家建议的在线学习问题：把每个候选核视为一个GP“专家”，用AdaHedge根据以拟合函数能力和与任务目标一致性为代理的损失，在线更新专家上的分布。提出两种变体：(i) 高斯混合（MoG）；(ii) 类别采样（categorical sampling）。

Result: 理论方面，在损失间隔（loss-gap）条件下证明权重会集中到最优核上，且所得采集函数接近最优专家的采集函数。实验方面，在BO、LSE和BAL任务上相较平方指数核、Matérn-5/2核以及简单集成基线均表现出稳健的性能。

Conclusion: HACK GPs通过在线专家建议框架实现了自适应的核选择，兼具理论保证与跨任务的实证优势，可缓解标准核在设定错误下的次优问题。

Abstract: Gaussian Processes (GPs) are widely used as surrogates for black-box functions in sequential decision-making problems such as Bayesian optimization (BO), level set estimation (LSE), and Bayesian active learning (BAL). GP performance critically depends on kernels, and standard kernels can lead to suboptimal decisions under misspecification. To address this, we introduce HACK GPs (Hedge Adaptive Cumulative Kernels), a method that views kernel selection as an online learning with expert advice problem. HACK treats each candidate kernel as a GP "expert" and updates a distribution over experts online using AdaHedge, based on a loss received as a proxy for their ability to fit the function and align with the task objective. We provide two variants of HACK: (i) Mixture of Gaussians (MoG) and (ii) categorical sampling. We establish general guarantees showing that, under a loss-gap condition, the weight concentrates on the best kernel and the resulting acquisition function is close to that of the best expert. Empirically, we observe robust performance across BO, LSE, and BAL compared to standard kernels such as Squared Exponential and Matern-5/2, as well as simple ensemble baselines.

</details>


### [302] [REARL: A Closed-loop Autonomous Driving Simulation Enhancement Framework with Real Traffic Data and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19903)
*Xiaojun Bi,Jun Jiang,Yiwen Sun,Quanyi Ou,Ke Cheng,Mingjie Bi,Yexin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REARL，一个将真实交通数据与大语言模型（LLM）结合的闭环仿真增强框架，通过聚类真实数据构建代表性场景、滑动窗口检测分布偏差，并由LLM动态调整车辆决策，从而缩小仿真与真实交通的分布差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶开发依赖高保真仿真，但真实交通的复杂性难以还原。现有基于预设规则或静态数据回放的仿真器难以应对动态交通；CRITICAL虽用真实数据与LLM调整初始配置，但随仿真推进，模拟分布仍偏离真实交通。

Method: REARL构建闭环仿真增强框架：对真实交通数据进行聚类，以各聚类中心作为代表性场景，为LLM提供典型真实交通模式；采用定时滑动窗口检测器监测车辆速度分布与车辆对平均间距的偏差，若某指标超过阈值则由LLM调整车辆决策，否则保留原控制器；此外，LLM从交通快照中选取匹配的真实车辆，参照其真实动作对仿真车辆进行调制。

Result: 在受控的HighD高速公路场景下，与CRITICAL基线及基于PPO的学习基线相比，REARL将速度分布的Hellinger距离降至0.3067，平均间距的MAPE降至0.8371，同时实现时间车头时距（THW）为22.8575、换道率为0.0708。

Conclusion: REARL通过真实数据聚类、滑动窗口偏差检测与LLM闭环决策调整，有效缓解了仿真分布随rollout演化的漂移问题，在多项指标上优于规则/回放式与强化学习基线，验证了LLM在闭环交通仿真增强中的可行性。

Abstract: Accurate simulation is crucial for autonomous driving development, yet capturing real-world traffic complexity remains challenging. Existing simulators that rely on predefined rules or static data playback struggle with dynamic traffic. CRITICAL uses real traffic data and a large language model (LLM) to adjust the initial simulation configuration, but the simulated distribution still diverges from real traffic as the rollout evolves. We propose REARL, a closed-loop simulation enhancement framework that integrates real traffic data with LLMs. Real traffic data are clustered, and each cluster center is used as a representative scenario that provides typical real-world traffic patterns for the LLM. A timed sliding-window detector then monitors discrepancies in vehicle speed distribution and mean spacing between pairs of vehicles. If a metric exceeds a threshold, the LLM adjusts vehicle decision-making; otherwise the existing controller is kept. The LLM also selects a matching real vehicle from a traffic snapshot and modulates the simulated vehicle with reference to that real action. In a controlled HighD highway setting, compared with the CRITICAL baseline and a PPO-based learning baseline, REARL reduces the Hellinger distance for speed distributions to 0.3067 and the MAPE for mean spacing to 0.8371, while achieving a time headway (THW) of 22.8575 and a lane change rate of 0.0708.

</details>


### [303] [Digital Twins for Opinion Dynamics: A Generative LLM Framework for Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19913)
*Omran Berjawi,Giuseppe Fenza,Rida Khatoun,Sherali Zeadally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于"数字孪生"概念的意见动力学模拟框架：通过克隆真实Twitter网络、为智能体赋予角色/情绪/中心性/固执度/影响力等属性，并用Mistral-7B根据记忆与社会暴露执行意见更新；在COVID-19和2020美国大选两个真实数据集上验证，预测误差比最佳经典基线降低50%以上，并实现结构对齐与极化动态的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交网络意见动力学是计算社会科学的核心挑战，与政治极化、错误信息和健康响应理解直接相关。现有方法存在两类不足：一是简化的数学模型忽略了与信念更新相关的语言和语境因素；二是基于大语言模型（LLM）的模拟未针对真实数据进行验证。

Method: 提出基于"数字孪生"概念的意见动力学模拟框架：克隆真实Twitter交互网络；为智能体赋予一组属性（人设persona、情绪、中心性、固执度、影响力）；采用Mistral-7B，依据记忆（memory）与社会暴露（social exposure）执行意见更新。通过在两个真实Twitter数据集（COVID-19话语、2020年美国大选）上进行验证与消融实验来评估。

Result: 在COVID-19和2020美国大选数据集上，Mistral-7B分别达到MAE=0.150和0.121，相比最佳经典基线将个体预测误差降低超过50%。结构对齐指标Delta_r分别为0.120和0.180；极化动态指标Delta_Var分别为0.106和0.115，均取得类似改进。消融实验证实智能体属性、记忆和社会暴露均对预测保真度有贡献，其中智能体属性最关键。

Conclusion: 将Mistral-7B建立在经验性克隆的交互网络之上，可构建出能够复现复杂社会动态的真实感仿真框架。

Abstract: The study of opinion dynamics in social networks is one of the key challenges in computational social science with direct relevance to understanding political polarization, misinformation, and health responses. Current approaches focus on simplified mathematical models that ignore linguistic and contextual factors related to belief updates or use Large Language Model (LLM)-based simulations that have not been validated against real data. We present a framework based on the concept of a digital twin to simulate opinion dynamics in social networks. The approach fills the gap by cloning a real-world Twitter network, assigns a set of attributes for agents (such as persona, emotions, centrality, stubbornness, and influence), and employs Mistral-7B to perform opinion update based on memory and social exposure. To evaluate the proposed approach, we validate it against two real Twitter datasets (COVID-19 discourse and U.S elections 2020). The results show that the capability of the proposed framework reproduces opinion trajectories and reduces individual prediction error by more than 50% compared to the best-performing classical baseline (Mistral-7B achieves Mean Absolute Error (MAE) = 0.150 and 0.121 on the COVID-19 and US Election 2020 datasets, respectively). We observe similar improvements in structural alignment (Delta_r = 0.120 and 0.180) and polarization dynamics (Delta_Var = 0.106 and 0.115) on the two datasets, respectively. Additionally, the ablation studies confirm that agent attributes, memory, and social exposure all contribute to the framework's predictive fidelity in reproducing opinion trajectories, with agent attributes being the most critical contributor. Overall, our results demonstrate that grounding Mistral-7B within empirically cloned interaction networks produces a realistic simulation framework capable of reproducing complex social dynamics.

</details>


### [304] [Amortizing Physics-Informed Neural Solvers via Graph Hypernetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19915)
*Cheng Jing,Abhishek Verma,Kallol Bera,Yixuan He,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将PDE方程描述为算子图（字段、导数、项、残差为节点，系数为项属性），用图超网络生成对角码以初始化元训练的因式分解PINN，在无解标签条件下比较系数向量、DeepSets项集合与图条件三种描述方式，发现显式算子关系在双场耦合问题上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将PINN摊销到相关PDE时，需要向可复用求解器描述每个方程。现有系数向量仅在预定义槽位中编码数值参数，算子和跨字段的分配关系是隐式的，限制了求解器对结构不同方程的适应性。

Method: 构建算子图显式表达方程关系：节点表示字段、导数、项和残差，系数作为项的属性保留。图超网络生成对角码，初始化一个元训练的因式分解PINN。元训练与目标适配仅使用控制方程和规定条件，不使用解标签。在固定适配预算下，对比系数向量、基于DeepSets的项集合和图条件三种条件化方式。

Result: 在标量对流-扩散-反应问题中，两种基于项的表述均提升了高反应精度且表现相近。在双场Fisher-KPP中，元训练见未耦合和单向系统，对未见双向耦合经过3000步适配后，图条件的平均最终误差比项集合低35.7%，比系数向量低67.7%。在固定结构的容性耦合等离子体模型中，系数向量表现最佳。

Conclusion: 结果支持在基于物理的求解器适配中，用显式方程关系扩展系数条件化，尤其对具有跨场耦合结构的方程有效；但结构固定时简单系数向量仍具竞争力。

Abstract: Amortizing physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) across related PDEs requires describing each equation to a reusable solver. Coefficient vectors encode numerical parameters in predefined slots, leaving operator and cross-field assignments implicit. We make these relationships explicit in an operator graph, with nodes for fields, derivatives, terms, and residuals and coefficients retained as term attributes. A graph hypernetwork generates diagonal codes that initialize a meta-trained factorized PINN for each target equation. Meta-training and target-specific adaptation use governing equations and prescribed conditions without solution labels. We compare coefficient-vector, DeepSets-based term-set, and graph conditioning by solution accuracy within a fixed adaptation budget. In scalar convection-diffusion-reaction problems, both term-based descriptors improve high-reaction accuracy, with similar performance. In two-field Fisher-KPP, meta-training sees uncoupled and one-way systems; after 3,000 adaptation steps on unseen two-way coupling, the graph's mean final error is 35.7% below the term set and 67.7% below the coefficient vector. In a fixed-structure capacitively coupled plasma model, the coefficient vector performs best. These results support extending coefficient conditioning with explicit equation relationships for physics-based solver adaptation.

</details>


### [305] [One Intervention per Component is Enough: Towards Identifiability in Linear Stochastic Dynamics from Steady State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19955)
*Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从稳态观测与干预数据中恢复多元OU过程参数的问题。作者证明了每个强连通分量(SCC)一次干预即可在全局缩放因子意义下识别全部参数的条件，并提出递归学习算法与正则化最小二乘估计器，实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用（如大规模基因扰动实验）只能获得稳态"快照"测量，无法获得时间序列轨迹，因此依赖轨迹的SDE估计方法不适用，需要研究仅用稳态观测与干预数据恢复OU过程参数的方法。

Method: 首先建立可识别性结果：在SCC凝聚图连通且单一根、并满足一定谱非退化假设下，对漂移图每个SCC一次干预即可在全局缩放因子意义下恢复全部OU参数。随后提出递归学习算法：对SCC进行拓扑排序，对每个分量隔离其边缘动力学，利用已恢复的上游参数从稳态矩方程求解线性系统。最后提出正则化最小二乘估计器，联合最小化观测与干预数据下稳态均值方程和协方差方程的残差。

Result: 实验验证了所提理论，能够恢复底层OU过程的参数。

Conclusion: 稳态观测与干预数据足以在合理假设下识别OU过程参数，所提递归算法与正则化最小二乘估计器有效，为无法获取时间序列的应用场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of recovering the parameters of a multivariate Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (OU) process from steady-state observational and interventional data. In many applications, such as large-scale gene perturbation experiments, only stationary "snapshot" measurements are available, making standard stochastic differential equation estimation methods that rely on time-series trajectories inapplicable. We first establish an identifiability result: one intervention per strongly connected component (SCC) of the drift graph suffices to recover all OU process parameters generically up to a global scaling factor. This holds provided that the SCC condensation graph is connected with a single root and certain spectral nondegeneracy assumptions hold. We propose a recursive learning algorithm that orders SCCs topologically and, for each component, isolates its marginal dynamics and solves a linear system derived from the steady-state moment equations, leveraging parameters recovered for upstream components. Building on this theoretical foundation, we propose a regularized least-squares estimator that jointly minimizes residuals of the steady-state mean and covariance equations across observational and interventional data. Experimental results validate our theoretical findings in recovering parameters of the underlying OU process.

</details>


### [306] [Graph-Based Stochastic Power-UCT: Monte-Carlo Graph Search with Power Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19956)
*Tung Tran,Viet Bao Mai,Hoang Ta,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出图结构的随机规划算法GS-Power-UCT及其全状态变体，通过共享同深度状态复用样本，提升随机MDP规划中的样本效率，并给出收敛性证明与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树结构MCTS在随机MDP中会将不同轨迹到达的同一状态重复建树，导致模拟样本浪费。作者希望在通用随机MDP（含环）中引入状态共享机制，以提升样本效率，同时保持收敛性保证。

Method: 提出GS-Power-UCT：在同一规划深度共享状态、不同深度保持独立值，适用于一般随机MDP及带环问题；进一步给出全状态变体GS-Power-UCT-F，每个物理状态仅存一个节点以增强样本共享，但会混合不同剩余视界值；GS-Power-UCT-F$^+$采用自适应视界控制偏差。理论分析固定规划视界下根估计的收敛率。

Result: 证明固定规划视界下根估计以$O(n^{-1/2})$速率收敛到有限视界值，与树结构Stochastic-Power-UCT相当，同时跨共享状态复用样本；GS-Power-UCT-F$^+$在跨深度差距消失时收敛到根状态$s_0$的最优无限视界折扣值$V^{\star}(s_0)$；随机规划基准实验显示相较树结构和图结构基线样本效率更优。

Conclusion: 图结构状态共享可有效减少随机MDP规划中的重复模拟，在保持理论收敛保证的同时提升样本效率；自适应视界设计可控制全状态共享带来的偏差，并扩展至无限视界折扣值收敛，为随机规划提供更高效的MCTS框架。

Abstract: Tree-based Monte-Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) duplicates the same state when it is reached through different trajectories, which can waste simulations in stochastic MDPs. We introduce Graph-Based Stochastic-Power-UCT (GS-Power-UCT), which shares states reached at the same planning depth while keeping separate values for states reached at different depths. This design applies to general stochastic MDPs, including problems with cycles. We prove that for a fixed planning horizon, the root estimate converges to the finite-horizon value at rate $O(n^{-1/2})$, matching tree-based Stochastic-Power-UCT while reusing samples across shared states. We also study two full-state variants: GS-Power-UCT-F, which stores one node per physical state to increase sample sharing but may mix values from different remaining horizons, and GS-Power-UCT-F$^+$, which uses an adaptive horizon to control this bias. The latter converges to $V^{\star}(s_0)$, the optimal infinite-horizon discounted value at the root state $s_0$, when the remaining cross-depth gap vanishes. Experiments on stochastic planning benchmarks show improved sample efficiency over tree-based and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [307] [CellRFT: Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Single-Cell Perturbation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19970)
*Jie Yan,Li Liu,Hanze Guo,Jiaxin Hu,Houxin He,Xiaoning Qi,Haoran Wang,Cong Li,Zhong-Yuan Zhang,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CellRFT强化微调框架，将生物学评价指标作为直接训练反馈，通过策略梯度与分层奖励聚合提升扰动预测并揭示指标间关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞扰动建模中，现有模型优化的是代理损失（surrogate loss），与评价所用的生物学标准不一致，导致数据拟合更好并不必然带来更好的生物学预测，存在目标错配问题。

Method: CellRFT采用强化微调框架，利用策略梯度优化从生成细胞群的非可微评价中学习，并通过分层奖励聚合整合多种生物学奖励。

Result: 在多种预训练模型上验证了适用性，有效提升扰动预测；发现优化某一生物学标准可能促进或损害其他标准，且互补奖励能改善未被直接优化的指标。

Conclusion: CellRFT为探测生物学指标如何塑造模型行为提供了途径，有望指导评价体系设计。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to perturbations supports the study of gene function, disease mechanisms, and therapeutic strategies. Despite advances in single-cell perturbation modeling, existing models typically optimize surrogate losses that do not directly reflect the biological criteria used for evaluation, so better data fitting need not yield better biological predictions. To address this mismatch, we introduce \textbf{CellRFT}, a reinforcement fine-tuning framework that uses biological evaluation as direct training feedback. CellRFT uses policy-gradient optimization to learn from non-differentiable evaluations of generated cell populations and integrates multiple biological rewards through hierarchical reward aggregation. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate CellRFT's applicability across different pretrained models and effectiveness in improving perturbation prediction, reveal that optimizing one biological criterion can help or hinder others, and show that complementary rewards can improve criteria beyond those directly optimized, offering a way to probe how biological metrics shape model behavior, with the potential to inform evaluation design. Code will be made available.

</details>


### [308] [Past, Future, All at Once: Mitigating Stability-Plasticity Dilemma via Post-hoc JANUS Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19985)
*Zhilong Zheng,Letian Tao,Yang Guan,Yujie Yang,Wei Xiong,Kehua Sheng,Bo Zhang,Jingliang Duan,Keqiang Li,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出JANUS框架，通过将参数更新投影到雅可比零空间实现参数空间正交，以事后、与调优方法无关的方式缓解微调中的灾难性遗忘，并兼顾效率与稳定性-可塑性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基础模型上进行新任务微调时不可避免地发生灾难性遗忘。现有基于参数高效微调的方法虽试图缓解该问题，但采用了过于严苛的子空间正交条件，限制了效果。

Method: 提出纯事后（post-hoc）、与调优无关的权重矫正框架，实现参数空间正交——这是保持历史性能的一阶必要充分条件。将参数更新投影到雅可比零空间（JANUS），在不干扰底层微调过程的前提下恢复历史知识。为克服雅可比近似的局部有效性，进一步提出多步自适应矫正机制，利用JANUS偏移动态验证有效信任域并调整步长。并结合ghost projection、ghost orientation comparison和序列级SVD压缩技术提升时空效率。

Result: 实验表明JANUS可与多种微调方法无缝集成，在保持下游任务适应的同时恢复历史知识，显著缓解稳定性-可塑性困境。

Conclusion: JANUS作为一种事后、与调优无关的通用框架，通过雅可比零空间投影实现参数空间正交，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，并具备良好的时空效率与通用性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning foundation models on new tasks inevitably suffer from catastrophic forgetting. While existing works attempt to mitigate this on the basis of parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, they adopted an overly restrictive Subspace Orthogonality condition. In this paper, we introduce a purely post-hoc and tuning-agnostic weight rectification framework that achieves Parameter Space Orthogonality, which is the necessary and sufficient condition for preserving historical performance to the first order. By projecting parameter updates into the JAcobian NUll Space (JANUS), our method significantly recovers compromised historical knowledge without interfering with the underlying fine-tuning process. To overcome the local validity of the Jacobian approximation, we further propose a Multi-step Adaptive Rectification mechanism that utilizes the JANUS shift to dynamically verify the valid trust region and adjust step sizes. Coupled with our proposed ghost projection, ghost orientation comparison, and sequence-level singular value decomposition compression techniques, JANUS also achieves great temporal and spatial efficiency. Experiments demonstrate that JANUS seamlessly integrates with various fine-tuning methods, significantly mitigating the stability-plasticity dilemma by recovering historical knowledge while preserving downstream task adaptation.

</details>


### [309] [EPIG-Tree: Compute-Optimal Branching for Gradient-Efficient Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20004)
*Nikita Khomich,Leopold Hermansson,Ido Hakimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将树状 rollout 构建视为策略梯度估计的计算分配问题，提出基于总方差分解的 EPIG-Tree 分支分配准则，在连续控制、LLM 梯度校准、单轮数学与多轮 Wordle 任务上验证其优于扁平 GRPO 与熵分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于奖励的强化学习（如 GRPO）将整条随机轨迹压缩为单一标量奖励，导致整个轨迹中所有 token 或动作共享同一轨迹级优势，探索与奖励分配效率低下：轨迹中大量因果决策、恢复尝试与环境随机性事件无法被区分，计算资源未用在最需要降低策略梯度不确定性的位置。

Method: 从局部策略梯度随机变量的全方差分解出发，推导两条分配规律：新增分支降低决策不确定性，重复后缀 rollout 降低延续不确定性。据此设计 EPIG-Tree 分数，利用已计算的 rollout 估计占用率与分数加权的价值不确定性，并给出后缀分配律 n_e ∝ w_e ‖∇_θ log π(a_e|h_e)‖ σ_e / √c_e，实现"以单位计算降低梯度不确定性最大"为原则的分支放置。

Result: 在克隆状态控制中，EPIG 降低梯度 MSE，在 13 个环境扫描中的 9 个稠密连续控制环境全部胜出，并近乎完美地恢复参考梯度方向；在冻结 LLM 上相对于熵分支改善了梯度校准。在线单轮数学任务中，树局部信用优于扁平 GRPO，但分支放置的影响次于 token 级信用分配。在线多轮 Wordle 中，EPIG 取得最高最终胜率 0.850，超过早期饱和于 0.790 的扁平 GRPO 以及熵分支。

Conclusion: 分支应放置在单位计算下最能降低策略梯度不确定性的位置，而非仅依赖策略不确定性；EPIG 的梯度估计优势可迁移到有状态、大动作空间的多轮任务，但在线单轮任务的收益更多来自 token 级信用分配而非分支放置。

Abstract: Reward-based reinforcement learning for language models, exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), collapses an entire stochastic trajectory into a single scalar reward. This is clean and scalable, but it explores and allocates reward inefficiently: a trajectory may contain many causal decisions, recovery attempts, and environment-randomness events, yet every token or action inherits one trajectory-level advantage. We study tree-based rollout construction as a compute-allocation problem for policy-gradient estimation. Our central claim is that branches should be placed not where the policy is merely uncertain, but where an additional branch most reduces uncertainty about the policy gradient per unit of compute. From a law-of-total-variance decomposition of the local policy-gradient random variable, we derive two allocation laws: new branches reduce decision uncertainty, while repeated suffix rollouts reduce continuation uncertainty. The resulting EPIG-Tree score allocates branches using the already computed rollouts. It estimates occupancy- and score-weighted value uncertainty, along with a suffix law $n_e \propto w_e \|\nabla_θ\log π(a_e|h_e)\| σ_e / \sqrt{c_e}$. Empirically, EPIG reduces gradient MSE in cloned-state control, winning in all nine dense continuous-control environments of a 13-environment sweep and recovering the reference gradient direction near-perfectly, and it improves frozen-LLM gradient calibration relative to entropy branching. In online single-turn math, tree-local credit beats flat GRPO, while branch placement is secondary to token-level credit assignment. In online multi-turn Wordle, EPIG attains the highest final win rate (0.850), overtaking flat GRPO, which saturates early at 0.790, and entropy branching as training proceeds, confirming that the gradient-estimation advantage transfers to a stateful, large-action setting.

</details>


### [310] [Dynamic Generalized Gromov-Wasserstein Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20008)
*Junda Ying,Zhiwei Zeng,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TP-DATE 框架，将 Gromov–Wasserstein 最优传输动态化，通过路径作用构建二次型 OT 并证明静态-动态等价，开发 travelling-pair flow matching 实现无仿真训练，在空间转录组数据上更好地保持空间结构并重建连续 3D 动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传输仅匹配表达分布，GW-OT 引入结构感知代价，适合空间转录组中需同时保持组织结构与匹配表达模式的对齐任务；但现有 GW-OT 多为静态形式，缺少可重建连续轨迹的通用动态表述。

Method: 1) 用路径作用（path actions）统一刻画一大类静态与动态二次型最优传输（QOT），并证明静态-动态等价；2) 提出 Travelling Pair Dynamical Alignment and Trajectory Estimation（TP-DATE），以无仿真方式将 GW-OT 动态化；3) 设计 travelling-pair flow matching，使条件路径间可相互作用，并将其交互边缘化聚合为单一向量场。

Result: 在合成数据与真实空间转录组数据上，TP-DATE 相较基线更好地保持空间结构，并提升连续 3D 动态重建质量。

Conclusion: TP-DATE 为结构感知最优传输提供了统一的静态-动态理论框架与可行的无仿真计算方案，验证了动态 GW-OT 在空间转录组连续轨迹重建中的有效性。

Abstract: Gromov--Wasserstein optimal transport (GW-OT) extends classical optimal transport by introducing structure-aware transport cost. This is particularly relevant for spatial transcriptomics, where dynamical reconstruction should preserve tissue structure in addition to matching expression patterns. While static formulations have been widely used for such structure-aware alignment, a general dynamic formulation for reconstructing continuous trajectories is still missing. We introduce Travelling Pair Dynamical Alignment and Trajectory Estimation (TP-DATE), a theoretical and computational framework to generalize GW-OT dynamically in a simulation-free manner. We formulate a broad class of static and dynamic Quadratic-form OT (QOT) through path actions and prove the static dynamic equivalence. We further develop travelling-pair flow matching, which allows interacting conditional paths and marginalizes their interactions into a single vector field. On synthetic and real spatial transcriptomics data, TP-DATE better preserves spatial structure and improves continuous 3D dynamics reconstruction.

</details>


### [311] [Correct Now, Insufficient Later: Auditing Update Sufficiency in Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20045)
*Guangzhe Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出“配对历史审计”方法考察记忆系统在后续更新下丢弃必要区分的能力缺陷，通过24对历史、6种机制、12种记忆条件、2次重复与2个模型后端的试点发现系统性失败，并提供记录级诊断与标签等变修复，但修复无法解决未知未来相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆可能在当前查询上回答正确，却丢弃了后续更新所需的区分信息；需要一种能揭示这类“当前正确但未来失效”的评测方法，并区分失败发生在保留状态、响应传递还是答案模式合规环节。

Method: 采用配对历史审计：两段历史当前答案相同，共享同一未来更新，但要求不同后续答案；试点覆盖24对历史、6种合成机制、12种记忆条件、2次重复、2个模型后端。使用确定性前沿选择器与结构化写入器，并配置四结果联合对比；进一步进行记录级审计（区分保留状态充分性、响应传递、答案模式合规）、墓碑移除测试、标识符重命名测试与标签等变修复。

Result: 前沿选择器严格揭示准确率为DeepSeek 96/96、GLM 82/96；结构化写入器62次成功（1项未决）与56/96。四结果联合对比的有限样本识别区间为[0.521, 0.542]与[0.292, 0.313]（非置信区间）。记录级审计发现26与25个格式良好但语义错误的结构化揭示记忆；GLM前沿揭示失败的14例均含正确值但封装错误。墓碑移除在目标机制中产生16/16精确重放失败。标识符重命名后，原始前沿后引用充分性从8/8降至94/320变换实例。标签等变修复仅保留2/8原始后引用答案。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种有边界的评测方法与可复现失败分析，而非所修复算法的普遍优越性。消除命名捷径并不能解决未知未来相关性；付费试点证据、回顾性诊断与新的离线测试分别报告，未声称独立留出集或自然任务验证。

Abstract: A memory can answer a current query correctly while discarding distinctions required by a later update. We investigate this failure with a paired-history audit: two histories have the same current answer, receive a shared future update, and require different subsequent answers. A pilot evaluates 24 history pairs across six synthetic mechanisms, 12 memory conditions, two repeats, and two model backends. A deterministic frontier selector obtains strict reveal accuracy of 96/96 on DeepSeek and 82/96 on GLM; a structured writer obtains 62 successes with one unresolved outcome and 56/96. The configured four-outcome joint contrast has finite-sample identification intervals of [0.521, 0.542] and [0.292, 0.313], not confidence intervals. A record-level audit distinguishes retained-state adequacy, response delivery, and answer-schema compliance without changing those original scores. It finds 26 and 25 well-formed but semantically wrong structured reveal memories, while all 14 GLM frontier reveal failures contain correct values in the wrong wrapper. Tombstone removal produces 16/16 exact replay failures in the targeted mechanism. Identifier renaming then exposes a separate flaw: original frontier late-reference adequacy falls from 8/8 to 94/320 transformed instances. We provide and test a label-equivariant repair, but it preserves only 2/8 original late-reference answers: eliminating a naming shortcut does not solve unknown future relevance. These results support a scoped evaluation methodology and reproducible failure analysis, not general superiority of the repaired algorithm. Paid pilot evidence, retrospective diagnostics, and new offline tests are reported separately; no independent held-out or natural-task validation is claimed.

</details>


### [312] [Evaluating Explanation Methods by the Predictors They Induce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20058)
*Jacob Selbæk,Hugo L. Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用"可否从解释重建模型预测"作为解释质量的可比性检验：把解释转化为可加预测器并在未见数据上评估其复现能力，无需拟合，得分即反映解释本身；在PDP、ALE、SHAP、LIME上验证，并结合理论与13个真实数据集、9个合成设计、4类模型家族的实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对机器学习模型解释的评价标准难以相互比较，缺乏统一、客观的衡量方式。作者希望用一个更简单且可操作的准则来评判解释是否真正刻画了模型对特征的利用方式。

Method: 提出"重建测试"：若解释真实描述模型如何使用特征，则应能据此重建模型预测。具体做法是读取每个特征的效应并相加，将解释转化为可加预测器，在未见数据上测量其对模型输出的复现程度；该过程不进行拟合，因此得分反映解释自身。方法适用于任何可写成特征函数的解释，并在PDP、ALE、SHAP、LIME上示范。理论上证明当特征独立时，求和部分依赖曲线给出模型最佳可加摘要；当特征相关时该性质失效。

Result: 在13个真实数据集、9个合成设计和4类模型家族上实验：哪种方法得分最佳完全取决于特征相关性。特征独立时SHAP略差于PDP，与理论预测一致；在相关的真实数据上SHAP领先。此外，一些广泛使用的质量指标甚至会偏好"被破坏的解释"而非"完好的解释"。

Conclusion: 以"能否重建模型预测"作为解释评价标准是可行且更具可比性的：它无需拟合、直接评估解释本身，并揭示方法优劣与特征相关性密切相关。同时说明现有部分常用质量指标存在缺陷，可能选出被损坏的解释。

Abstract: Explanations of machine learning models are usually judged by criteria that are hard to compare. We propose a simpler test: if an explanation really describes how a model uses its features, it should be possible to rebuild the model's predictions from it. We turn each explanation into a predictor by reading each feature's effect and adding them up, and measure how well that predictor reproduces the model on unseen data. Nothing is fitted, so the score reflects the explanation itself. The test applies to any explanation that can be written as a function of the features; we demonstrate it on partial dependence plots (PDP), accumulated local effects (ALE), SHAP and LIME. We prove that summing partial dependence curves gives the best possible additive summary of a model when its features are independent, and that this fails when they are dependent.
